# FORM 1-B — GM COPY. NEVER SHOW PLAYERS, NEVER SYNC.

Generated by `tools/induction-agreement/build.mjs` from `tools/induction-agreement/source.mjs`. Don't edit by hand.
Players see this agreement once, on page 2 of the induction terminal, and can't go on without signing.
It runs to 56,987 words (about 4 hours 45 minutes at 200 words a minute), 197 Articles and 40 Schedules.
Status: DRAFTED. Not to be shown to players until Ingram has read and graded it.

## Hook index (GM-only)

| Clause | Hook | What it's for |
|---|---|---|
| 68.5 | monitoring | The Chapter 8 reveal: the Directorate has been reading their messages all along, under the paperwork they signed on day one. Pairs with Intake Medicals' form 12b. |
| 87.3 | samples | Sets up the Gen 2 DNA piloting reveal around level 4 without saying what the tests find. Pairs with the blood screen at Intake Medicals. |
| 94.4 | likeness | Echoes the founders' 'famous names, sanitised faces' portraits: the Directorate can make anyone look like whatever it wants. Use when the party finds themselves on a recruitment poster, improved. |
| 98.4 | founders-portraits | The four founders' players will read this later and laugh. Pays off against Shucks the skeleton and the gnoll statue labelled HYUCK. |
| 48.6 | prototype-trials | Hyuck, the mad test pilot of the Directorate's worst Frame concepts, arrives with the paperwork already signed. Also any GM-invented ridiculous machine. |
| 15.6 | appearance-warranty | Every disguised character is in breach the moment they sign, and (GM-only) so is everyone, given the universal mitflit DNA. For comedy and the Gen 2 reveal. NEVER use it to force a player's reveal; the reveal is always the player's. |
| 12.4 | working-name | Callsigns and nicknames later; also a way to rename a PC in the fiction as a joke. |
| 44.3 | damage-deductions | Hook into the machine credit budget and repair backlog: the bill for a wrecked vehicle, itemised. |
| 54.3 | salvage | Strategy-layer artefacts and anything the party wants to keep: the Directorate can claim it. Also any residue from a site. |
| 58.4 | sealed-envelope | OPEN-ENDED. Hand a PC a sealed envelope at any point (Kit Issue, a briefing) and decide later what's in it. |
| 62.4 | custody | OPEN-ENDED. Something was put in a PC's locker, kitbag or machine. It's their responsibility now. |
| 76.5 | approved-account | The official line and approved accounts (LEDGER #27, #35): the Directorate rewrites what happened, and the party signed up to believe it. |
| 79.3 | forgetting | OPEN-ENDED and funny: a clerk handing a PC Form 9-F after they've seen something. Also a way to acknowledge a discovery without the Directorate admitting it. |
| 105.4 | police | The police and the Directorate distrust each other (LEDGER #35). Friction at a cordon; a PC caught talking to Hori. |
| 116.4 | any-section | Section Nine's roster, countersigned by a Medical officer who doesn't exist (Rand, LEDGER #34). Any mystery assignment later. |
| 120.4 | one-favour | OPEN-ENDED. The GM can call it in at any point, from anyone who can show the clause. |
| 123.3 | form-7v | OPEN-ENDED. A stranger with a Form 7-V can knock at midnight. Who issues Form 7-Vs is undecided. |
| 127.3 | biscuit | A reward for the one player who actually reads it. If anyone asks for the biscuit, honour it in the fiction (and at the table, if there are biscuits). |
| 130.3 | strays | Plants the stray (v4 §6: something small that follows one PC home, waiting to be fed) without saying what it is. |
| 133.4 | annex-q | OPEN-ENDED. Annex Q is blank. Fill it whenever the story needs a term the party has 'already agreed to'. |
| 136.4 | variation | Lets the Directorate change the rules mid-crisis, legally, in the fiction. |
| 140.4 | hearings | OPEN-ENDED satire: a disciplinary hearing whose verdict is already typed. |

## The text

## FORM 1-B — ARTICLES OF ENROLMENT AND GENERAL CONSENTS

*RECRUIT GRADE (CLEARANCE 0)*

*(together with the Schedules, Annexes, Appendices, Standing Orders and Forms referred to herein, each of which forms part of this Agreement whether or not it is attached, has been printed, or exists at the date of signature)*

*Issued by the Induction Office, Kurose, under the authority of the Directorate*

### PARTIES

THIS AGREEMENT is made on the date shown on the terminal clock, which the parties agree is correct.

BETWEEN:

(1) THE DIRECTORATE, acting by its Induction Office and by any other Office it may from time to time decide was acting on its behalf (the "Directorate"); and

(2) THE PERSON OPERATING THIS TERMINAL, and any person the Directorate reasonably believes to have been operating it (the "Recruit").

### RECITALS

(A) The Directorate exists to keep a promise, and keeps it.

(B) The Recruit has applied, or has been streamed, or has otherwise arrived, and wishes to be enrolled.

(C) The Directorate is willing to enrol the Recruit on the terms set out below and on no others, save such others as may be added.

(D) Each party has read this Agreement in full, or has had a reasonable opportunity to do so, which the parties agree amounts to the same thing.

(E) The Recruit has been advised to seek independent advice and has declined, which declining is recorded.

(F) The parties wish to record their understanding in writing, at some length.

(G) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a raincoat, and that the issue be recorded.

(H) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the length of string, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 4.

(I) The Lost Property Office wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(J) The Office of Forms has, on 5 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex X.

(K) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the right boot, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 2.

(L) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a locker key, and that the issue be recorded.

(M) The Records Office and the Motor Pool have agreed which of them is responsible for the ration card, and have recorded that agreement on Form 25-X.

(N) The Restricted Library and the Boiler House have agreed which of them is responsible for the HB pencil, and have recorded that agreement on Form 96-D.

(O) The Dormitory Wardens has, on 3 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex H.

(P) The Medical Division and the Induction Office have agreed which of them is responsible for the copy of this Agreement, and have recorded that agreement on Form 76-H.

(Q) The Duty Office wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(R) The Motor Pool wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(S) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the pair of regulation socks, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 4.

(T) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a copy of this Agreement, and that the issue be recorded.

(U) The Board of Review and the Restricted Library have agreed which of them is responsible for the slide rule, and have recorded that agreement on Form 36-C.

(V) The Inspectorate of Queues and the Time Office have agreed which of them is responsible for the tray, and have recorded that agreement on Form 62-G.

(W) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the timetable, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 33.

(X) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the eraser, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 11.

(Y) The Dormitory Wardens wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(Z) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a fire bucket, and that the issue be recorded.

(AA) The Boiler House has, on 5 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex S.

(AB) The Signals Office wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(AC) The Inspectorate of Queues wishes to record that it is open, save when it is not.

(AD) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with an HB pencil, and that the issue be recorded.

(AE) The Office of Duplicate Keys has, on 7 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex F.

(AF) The Medical Division and the Hangar Office have agreed which of them is responsible for the fork, and have recorded that agreement on Form 98-Z.

(AG) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the stick of chalk, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 29.

(AH) The Boiler House has, on 6 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex V.

(AI) The Office of Duplicate Keys has, on 5 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex P.

(AJ) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a blanket, and that the issue be recorded.

(AK) It is expedient that every Recruit be issued with a whistle, and that the issue be recorded.

(AL) The Inspectorate of Queues has, on 8 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex G.

(AM) Certain Recruits have in the past been uncertain as to the status of the sewing kit, and this Agreement resolves that uncertainty in Schedule 28.

(AN) The Laundry has, on 7 occasions, drawn the attention of Recruits to Annex D.

NOW IT IS AGREED as follows.

### PART I — DEFINITIONS AND INTERPRETATION

**Article 1. Definitions**

1.1 In this Agreement, unless the context otherwise requires, and in some cases even where it does not:

(a) "Agreement" means this instrument, including every Schedule, Annex, Appendix, Standing Order and Form referred to in it, as amended, supplemented, reissued or reinterpreted from time to time;

(b) "Allowance" means any rubber stamp or thing that the Induction Office reasonably considers to be one;

(c) "Annex" has the meaning given to it by the Quartermaster's Stores, as amended;

(d) "Appendix" means the item so called by the Office of Ceremonies at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(e) "Approved Account" means the account of an event recorded by the Office of Approved Accounts, which is the account of that event;

(f) "Assignment" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(g) "Badge" means that which is recorded as such by the Hangar Office;

(h) "Board" means that which is recorded as such by the Office of Ceremonies;

(i) "Boot" means the item so called by the Records Office at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(j) "Briefing" means the item so called by the Quartermaster's Stores at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(k) "Bulletin" has the meaning given to it by the Post Room, as amended;

(l) "Callsign" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(m) "Canteen" means the Directorate canteen and any place in which the Canteen Committee is sitting;

(n) "Canteen Chit" means any mess tin or thing that the Motor Pool reasonably considers to be one;

(o) "Chit" means that which is recorded as such by the Office of Approved Accounts;

(p) "Clearance" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(q) "Clipboard" shall be construed in accordance with Schedule 33;

(r) "Corridor" means any kitbag or thing that the Laundry reasonably considers to be one;

(s) "Countersignature" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(t) "Day" means a period of four Quarters, whether or not the sun agrees;

(u) "Debrief" means that which is recorded as such by the Board of Review;

(v) "Deduction" means any stepladder or thing that the Laundry reasonably considers to be one;

(w) "Designation" shall be construed in accordance with Schedule 17;

(x) "Directorate Equipment" means any object issued to, lent to, left near, or used by the Recruit, together with anything written, stored or recorded on or by it;

(y) "Discharge" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(z) "Dormitory" shall be construed in accordance with Schedule 10;

(aa) "Drill" has the meaning given to it by the Induction Office, as amended;

(ab) "Duplicate" means that which is recorded as such by the Duty Office;

(ac) "Duty" means anything the Recruit is asked to do by a person who appears to be entitled to ask;

(ad) "Duty Officer" means any lanyard or thing that the Records Office reasonably considers to be one;

(ae) "Duty Roster" means any pair of gloves or thing that the Dormitory Wardens reasonably considers to be one;

(af) "Envelope" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 26-W;

(ag) "Exercise" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 37-W;

(ah) "Form" means that which is recorded as such by the Boiler House;

(ai) "Fortnight" means the period shown on the Time Office board as a fortnight;

(aj) "Founders" means the four persons honoured as the Founders of the Directorate, as depicted in the official portraits;

(ak) "Hatch" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 96-D;

(al) "Hearing" means the item so called by the Post Room at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(am) "Hours" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(an) "Inspection" means the item so called by the Restricted Library at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(ao) "Issue" means that which is recorded as such by the Correspondence Office;

(ap) "Kitbag" shall be construed in accordance with Schedule 26;

(aq) "Ledger" means any right boot or thing that the Office of Ceremonies reasonably considers to be one;

(ar) "Liaison" means the item so called by the Office of Forms at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(as) "Locker" means the item so called by the Office of Forms at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(at) "Machine" means any Frame, vehicle, rig, crane, conveyance or apparatus, whether or not it has been given a name, and whether or not it works;

(au) "Meal" means anything served in the Canteen during a mealtime, including on Thursdays;

(av) "Mealtime" means the item so called by the Signals Office at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(aw) "Muster" means that which is recorded as such by the Inspectorate of Queues;

(ax) "Notice" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(ay) "Noticeboard" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(az) "Office" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(ba) "Parade" means the item so called by the Time Office at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bb) "Pass" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 36-E;

(bc) "Pencil" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 65-P;

(bd) "Permit" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(be) "Posting" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(bf) "Precedence" means the item so called by the Works Department at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bg) "Probation" means any raincoat or thing that the Signals Office reasonably considers to be one;

(bh) "Quarter" means one of the four divisions of a Day, as recorded by the Time Office;

(bi) "Queue" has the meaning given to it by the Uniform Store, as amended;

(bj) "Receipt" means the item so called by the Quartermaster's Stores at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bk) "Record" means any record, whether or not accurate, which shall be treated as accurate;

(bl) "Requisition" shall be construed in accordance with Schedule 7;

(bm) "Roster" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(bn) "Rota" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(bo) "Sample" means any quantity of blood, tissue, hair, saliva or other material taken from, shed by, or otherwise obtained from the Recruit;

(bp) "Schedule" means that which is recorded as such by the Time Office;

(bq) "Seal" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(br) "Section" means any section of the Directorate, including sections not listed in Schedule 2, sections not yet formed, and sections the Recruit has not been told about;

(bs) "Sock" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(bt) "Stamp" means the item so called by the Time Office at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bu) "Standing Order" means the item so called by the Time Office at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bv) "Stationery" means the item so called by the Office of Ceremonies at the relevant time, whatever it was called at any other time;

(bw) "Stores" means any tea mug or thing that the Laundry reasonably considers to be one;

(bx) "Tea Urn" means the tea urn, which is Directorate property and is not a place;

(by) "Tray" means that which is recorded as such by the Signals Office;

(bz) "Triplicate" includes the plural, the singular, and any intermediate quantity;

(ca) "Uniform" has the meaning it had on the last occasion it was defined, unless redefined;

(cb) "Voucher" means that which is recorded as such by the Liaison Office;

(cc) "Waiting Room" includes any part of it, anything of which it forms part, and anything described as such on Form 7-G;

(cd) "Witness" means that which is recorded as such by the Induction Office;

**Article 2. Interpretation**

2.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no notebook is lost whether or not the Laundry is open;

(b) keep every torch battery under a bunk only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(c) report any typewriter ribbon that appears to be in store to the Correspondence Office; and

(d) ensure that no locker key is lost during any Quarter other than the first.

2.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every rubber stamp near the Tea Urn only, subject always to Schedule 37;

(b) not decline to carry any bucket promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) decline to carry each flask to the reasonable satisfaction of the Induction Office; and

(d) ensure that no length of string is damaged in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

2.3 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Approved Accounts for a replacement coat hook, which the Office of Approved Accounts may grant, refuse or defer, save as provided in Appendix 2.

2.4 A reference in this Article to the Induction Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Induction Office's hatch.

2.5 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a canteen chit is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

2.6 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any bucket, and a receipt for the receipt.

2.7 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any eraser, and a receipt for the receipt.

2.8 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a tray is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

2.9 Where this Article and Annex M conflict, Annex M shall prevail, save where it does not.

**Article 3. Precedence of Documents**

3.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 15.6(e) and Schedule 25.

3.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) store each black umbrella within one Quarter of being asked;

(b) keep every whistle in the Canteen only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) ensure that no pair of ear defenders is obsolete on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Ceremonies's discretion; and

(d) not polish any sewing kit before the next muster.

3.3 The Recruit may apply to the Board of Review for a replacement paperclip, which the Board of Review may grant, refuse or defer, except on alternate Tuesdays.

3.4 Where any two documents forming part of this Agreement conflict, the later shall prevail, unless the earlier is stamped, in which case the stamp shall prevail.

3.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) retain each butter knife unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) keep every notebook outside the hatch only, where practicable;

(c) ensure that no paperclip is undamaged but suspected save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(d) keep every pair of goggles in the Canteen only, in accordance with Annex L; and

(e) polish each identity disc save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

3.6 Should the Board of Review be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, except on alternate Tuesdays.

3.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any clipboard that appears to be surplus to the Board of Review;

(b) stack each flask save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(c) not label any raincoat by the end of the Fortnight.

3.8 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any right boot, and a receipt for the receipt.

### PART II — ENROLMENT

**Article 4. Enrolment**

4.1 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 5-J, within one Quarter of being asked.

4.2 The Office of Approved Accounts reserves the right to requisition any coat hanger at any time, and to decline to say why.

4.3 Any dispute as to whether a shoe brush is out of store shall be referred to the Records Office, whose decision shall be final, subject always to Schedule 4.

4.4 The Recruit is enrolled with effect from the moment of signature, or from such earlier moment as the Directorate may later determine.

4.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no message pad is in store in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(b) report any butter knife that appears to be accounted for twice to the Induction Office; and

(c) not present any torch before the next muster.

4.6 Enrolment is not employment, apprenticeship, tenancy or friendship, and shall not be construed as any of them unless the Directorate so construes it.

4.7 Any dispute as to whether a tray is undamaged but suspected shall be referred to the Inspectorate of Queues, whose decision shall be final, within one Quarter of being asked.

4.8 Any blanket not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Liaison Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 5.

4.9 The Recruit shall not return a timetable on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

**Article 5. Seals (Continued)**

5.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) dust each left boot if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(b) re-requisition each name tape on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Induction Office's discretion;

(c) keep every left boot outside the hatch only, during any Quarter other than the first; and

(d) report any sewing kit that appears to be reissued to the Quartermaster's Stores.

5.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every helmet in a Machine only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) account for each eraser within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) keep every slide rule in the Canteen only, within one Quarter of being asked; and

(d) ensure that no tin of boot polish is undamaged but suspected during any Quarter other than the first.

5.3 Where the Recruit fails to account for a mop, the Boiler House may treat that mop as out of store, and Clause 185.6(a) shall apply as if it had been surplus.

5.4 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 80-W, where practicable.

5.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no ink pad is undamaged but suspected notwithstanding Clause 128.1(d);

(b) not stack any black umbrella before the next muster;

(c) report any left boot that appears to be reissued to the Stationery Office; and

(d) keep every left boot in a corridor only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

5.6 A slide rule that has been found for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

5.7 The Office of Keys may delegate any function under this Article to the Signals Office, which may delegate it back.

**Article 6. Stamps**

6.1 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any notebook, and a receipt for the receipt.

6.2 A clipboard that has been in transit for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, whether or not the Inspectorate of Queues is open.

6.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 71.3 and Schedule 24.

6.4 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 142.5 and Schedule 22.

6.5 A reference in this Article to the Restricted Library includes a reference to any person standing at the Restricted Library's hatch.

6.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no lanyard is found unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) present each black umbrella if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(c) keep every eraser in a Machine only, where practicable; and

(d) stack each flask on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Signals Office's discretion.

6.7 The Office of Duplicate Keys may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

6.8 The Recruit's signature on Form 53-H is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 53-H, whether or not Form 53-H was attached.

6.9 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hook is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 7. Waiting Rooms (Further Provisions)**

7.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one rubber stamp into the Office of Keys at any one time.

7.2 Where the Recruit fails to fold a rubber stamp, the Quartermaster's Stores may treat that rubber stamp as unaccounted for, and Clause 197.1(e) shall apply as if it had been in transit.

7.3 The Recruit acknowledges that the Works Department is not responsible for any slide rule left under a bunk.

7.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every butter knife under a bunk only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) keep every stick of chalk on the stairs only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) not keep dry any tin of boot polish by the end of the Fortnight; and

(d) not report the loss of any ink pad on the same Day.

7.5 The Recruit shall report any lanyard found on the parade ground to the Office of Ceremonies on the same Day.

7.6 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 86.4, Clause 49.2(e) and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

7.7 No blanket shall be treated as undamaged but suspected without the prior written consent of the Records Office, whether or not the Records Office is open.

**Article 8. Grade and Clearance**

8.1 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 152.6, Clause 137.6 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

8.2 Where the Recruit fails to stack an oil can, the Lost Property Office may treat that oil can as found, and Clause 112.5(c) shall apply as if it had been unaccounted for.

8.3 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mop into the Board of Review at any one time.

8.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one eraser into the Motor Pool at any one time.

8.5 The Recruit holds the grade of Recruit and Clearance 0 until notified otherwise on the appropriate Form.

8.6 The Quartermaster's Stores may, subject always to Schedule 21, require the Recruit to sign for any torch.

8.7 A Recruit who believes they have been promoted shall continue to act as a Recruit until the notice of promotion has been stamped.

8.8 No butter knife shall be treated as obsolete without the prior written consent of the Records Office, except on alternate Tuesdays.

8.9 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Induction Office to retain any timetable, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 9. Permits (Supplementary)**

9.1 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Dormitory Wardens to polish any typewriter ribbon, without prejudice to Article 154.

9.2 Should the Stationery Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Stationery Office.

9.3 Should the Induction Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, during any Quarter other than the first.

9.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one slide rule into the Office of Approved Accounts at any one time.

9.5 The Recruit shall report any eraser found in a Waiting Room to the Time Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

9.6 A shoe brush returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

9.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 19-V, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 10. Passes (Further Provisions)**

10.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) stack each mess tin whether or not the Inspectorate of Queues is open;

(b) not store any length of string before the next muster;

(c) keep every butter knife on a windowsill only, save as provided in Appendix 3; and

(d) ensure that no set of overalls is provisionally obsolete during any Quarter other than the first.

10.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to sign for more than one locker key at a time, save during an Exercise.

10.3 The Recruit acknowledges that the Hangar Office is not responsible for any pair of ear defenders left on a windowsill.

10.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not retain any blanket on the same Day;

(b) keep in a cool place each tray except on alternate Tuesdays;

(c) inspect each pair of goggles whether or not the Inspectorate of Queues is open; and

(d) report any drawing pin that appears to be out of store to the Boiler House.

10.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep in a cool place each pair of gloves during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) report any timetable that appears to be in store to the Inspectorate of Queues; and

(c) ensure that no fork is reissued notwithstanding Clause 31.2(iii).

**Article 11. Receipts (Supplementary)**

11.1 The Signals Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

11.2 The Recruit shall not carry more than one ink pad into the Lost Property Office at any one time.

11.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) not surrender any rubber stamp before the next muster;

(b) not decline to carry any notebook on the same Day;

(c) not surrender any locker key on the same Day;

(d) ensure that no length of string is undamaged but suspected subject always to Schedule 10;

(e) keep every torch battery on the stairs only, subject always to Schedule 17; and

(f) keep every fire bucket in a corridor only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

11.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any canteen chit that appears to be accounted for twice to the Stationery Office;

(b) report any locker key that appears to be obsolete to the Board of Review;

(c) not store any ruler on the same Day;

(d) dust each ruler provided that Form 38-V has been lodged in triplicate; and

(e) not count any notebook by the end of the Fortnight.

11.5 The Recruit shall not carry more than one slide rule into the Inspectorate of Queues at any one time.

11.6 Where this Article and Annex B conflict, Annex B shall prevail, save where it does not.

11.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) not decline to carry any dormitory key within one Quarter;

(b) report any kitbag that appears to be obsolete to the Dormitory Wardens;

(c) not keep in a cool place any identity disc on the same Day;

(d) keep every ruler in a Waiting Room only, without prejudice to Article 191; and

(e) store each oil can in accordance with Annex E.

**Article 12. Designations**

12.1 A fork that has been in store for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed provisionally obsolete, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

12.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every HB pencil in a Waiting Room only, in accordance with Annex W;

(b) inspect each clipboard within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) ensure that no copy of this Agreement is in transit unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(d) ensure that no message pad is undamaged but suspected to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board of Review;

(e) not polish any locker key before the next muster; and

(f) keep every black umbrella outside the hatch only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

12.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no pillow is accounted for twice within one Quarter of being asked;

(b) retain each stepladder in accordance with Annex K;

(c) report any length of string that appears to be in transit to the Induction Office;

(d) ensure that no coat hook is reissued save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(e) not surrender any butter knife before the next muster; and

(f) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each raincoat in accordance with Annex R.

12.4 The Directorate may assign the Recruit a working name, callsign or designation, which the Recruit shall answer to, and may change it without notice, and the Recruit shall answer to the new one as though it had always been so.

12.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 161.2, Clause 10.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

12.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) not carry any butter knife within one Quarter;

(b) keep every ration card under a bunk only, before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(c) label each mess tin unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

12.7 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Forms for a replacement 2B pencil, which the Office of Forms may grant, refuse or defer, save as provided in Appendix 2.

12.8 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any length of string, and a receipt for the receipt.

12.9 A designation is not a nickname. A nickname is not a designation. Neither is a rank.

12.10 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any torch battery that appears to be accounted for twice to the Stationery Office;

(b) keep every HB pencil on the stairs only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(c) report any helmet that appears to be obsolete to the Office of Ceremonies; and

(d) present each canteen chit notwithstanding Clause 173.6.

12.11 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to count more than one spoon at a time, save during an Exercise.

12.12 No sheet of carbon paper shall be treated as reissued without the prior written consent of the Office of Forms, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

**Article 13. Inspections (Supplementary)**

13.1 Where this Article and Annex Y conflict, Annex Y shall prevail, save where it does not.

13.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to present more than one bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

13.3 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to store more than one mop at a time, save during an Exercise.

13.4 A torch battery returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

13.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 149.5, Clause 75.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

13.6 Time spent queueing at the Inspectorate of Queues counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 14. Surplus Property**

14.1 Time spent queueing at the Correspondence Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

14.2 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any flask, and a receipt for the receipt.

14.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) requisition each stick of chalk provided that Form 36-E has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) not polish any coat hook promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(c) report any black umbrella that appears to be in transit to the Office of Approved Accounts.

14.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not retain any pair of gloves promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) keep every eraser in a Machine only, provided that Form 9-F has been lodged in triplicate;

(c) keep every fork on a windowsill only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(d) not polish any pillow before the next muster;

(e) keep every pair of regulation socks on the parade ground only, notwithstanding Clause 102.3; and

(f) ensure that no mess tin is reissued within one Quarter of being asked.

14.5 Where a blanket is found to be in transit, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

14.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a name tape is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

14.7 The Recruit may apply to the Hangar Office for a replacement fork, which the Hangar Office may grant, refuse or defer, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 15. Warranties**

15.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Laundry is not responsible for any black umbrella left outside the hatch.

15.2 A stepladder that has been found for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Time Office.

15.3 Time spent queueing at the Dormitory Wardens counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

15.4 The Recruit warrants that everything entered on Form 1-A is true and complete, and that any part left blank was left blank truthfully.

15.5 The Recruit shall dust every oil can issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 3.

15.6 The Recruit further warrants that the Recruit is, in every material respect, what the Recruit appears to be, and undertakes to notify the Induction Office in writing, within one Quarter, of any respect in which this warranty is or becomes untrue.

15.7 The Recruit acknowledges that the Lost Property Office is not responsible for any message pad left in a Waiting Room.

15.8 The Records Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

15.9 Should the Inspectorate of Queues be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Inspectorate of Queues.

**Article 16. Queues (Supplementary) (Supplementary)**

16.1 The Recruit shall report any torch found in a corridor to the Medical Division promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

16.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any canteen chit that appears to be surplus to the Board of Review;

(b) requisition each 2B pencil save as provided in Appendix 2;

(c) ensure that no set of overalls is found in accordance with Annex Z;

(d) not retain any mess tin within one Quarter; and

(e) not return any pillow by the end of the Fortnight.

16.3 No identity disc shall be treated as damaged without the prior written consent of the Restricted Library, during any Quarter other than the first.

16.4 Any dispute as to whether a 2B pencil is unaccounted for shall be referred to the Office of Approved Accounts, whose decision shall be final, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion.

16.5 The Recruit shall not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee an oil can on behalf of another Recruit, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

16.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hanger is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

16.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) not polish any flask before the next muster;

(b) not label any whistle within one Quarter;

(c) ensure that no mop is found on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Duplicate Keys's discretion;

(d) ensure that no pair of regulation socks is obsolete in accordance with Annex S;

(e) ensure that no ruler is lost in accordance with Annex P; and

(f) report any HB pencil that appears to be obsolete to the Office of Keys.

16.8 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 185.4(a) and Schedule 19.

**Article 17. Duplicates (Supplementary)**

17.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) not present any copy of this Agreement by the end of the Fortnight;

(b) report any spoon that appears to be pending inspection to the Boiler House;

(c) store each ruler whether or not the Duty Office is open;

(d) not report the loss of any mess tin within one Quarter;

(e) decline to carry each black umbrella in accordance with Annex O; and

(f) not present any notebook within one Quarter.

17.2 Where a fork is found to be out of store, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

17.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 146.2(d), Clause 70.6(iv) and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

17.4 Any dispute as to whether a butter knife is found shall be referred to the Lost Property Office, whose decision shall be final, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

17.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to re-requisition more than one left boot at a time, save during an Exercise.

17.6 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any butter knife, and a receipt for the receipt.

### PART III — DUTIES OF THE RECRUIT

**Article 18. General Duties**

18.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) requisition each pillow if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(b) keep every pair of ear defenders under a bunk only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) ensure that no butter knife is lost save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(d) ensure that no length of string is in store on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Inspectorate of Queues's discretion; and

(e) report the loss of each fire bucket except on alternate Tuesdays.

18.2 For the purposes of this Article, a notebook includes any part of a notebook, any object of which a notebook forms part, and anything described as a notebook on Form 54-T.

18.3 Any ruler not collected before the next muster shall pass to the Records Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 38.

18.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any notebook that appears to be in store to the Induction Office;

(b) keep every sewing kit near the Tea Urn only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Ceremonies; and

(c) report any right boot that appears to be accounted for twice to the Office of Duplicate Keys.

18.5 The Recruit shall perform every Duty diligently, cheerfully and in the correct order.

18.6 The Motor Pool reserves the right to inspect any shoe brush at any time, and to decline to say why.

18.7 An eraser that has been undamaged but suspected for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed surplus, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

18.8 Where two Duties conflict, the Recruit shall perform the Duty assigned by the more senior Office, as listed in Schedule 2, and shall apologise to the other.

18.9 Time spent queueing at the Office of Forms counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 19. Surplus Property**

19.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Keys is not responsible for any kitbag left near the Tea Urn.

19.2 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a fire bucket is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

19.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no bucket is obsolete without prejudice to Article 34;

(b) keep every sheet of carbon paper in the Canteen only, whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open;

(c) report any drawing pin that appears to be damaged to the Office of Approved Accounts; and

(d) not keep dry any spanner before the next muster.

19.4 The Recruit shall report any 2B pencil found under a bunk to the Stationery Office on the same Day.

19.5 The Recruit shall report any torch battery found in a Machine to the Records Office before the next muster.

19.6 For the purposes of this Article, a helmet includes any part of a helmet, any object of which a helmet forms part, and anything described as a helmet on Form 49-K.

19.7 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a left boot is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 20. Seals (Supplementary)**

20.1 The Inspectorate of Queues may, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit, require the Recruit to keep in a cool place any protractor.

20.2 The Board of Review may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

20.3 A tray returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

20.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a message pad is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

20.5 A reference in this Article to the Correspondence Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Correspondence Office's hatch.

20.6 A reference in this Article to the Boiler House includes a reference to any person standing at the Boiler House's hatch.

20.7 A torch returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

20.8 Any coat hook not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 12.

20.9 Any ink pad not collected before the next muster shall pass to the Office of Approved Accounts, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 31.

**Article 21. The Board and the Quarters**

21.1 Where a ruler is found to be accounted for twice, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

21.2 The Induction Office may, during any Quarter other than the first, require the Recruit to keep in a cool place any sheet of carbon paper.

21.3 The Recruit shall be findable, in each Quarter, in the place recorded against the Recruit's name on the Time Office board.

21.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any fork that appears to be accounted for twice to the Office of Forms;

(b) keep every sheet of carbon paper near the Tea Urn only, without prejudice to Article 116;

(c) not stack any ink pad promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(d) ensure that no fork is reissued if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(e) ensure that no sheet of carbon paper is accounted for twice within one Quarter of being asked; and

(f) not stack any blanket within one Quarter.

21.5 The Recruit may do more than one thing in a Quarter, but may be recorded as being in only one place, and shall be in that place if anybody looks.

21.6 A locker key returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

21.7 A reference in this Article to the Liaison Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Liaison Office's hatch.

**Article 22. Ledgers (Continued)**

22.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 154.2(a)(iii) and Schedule 15.

22.2 The Recruit shall report any helmet found on a windowsill to the Boiler House on the same Day.

22.3 For the purposes of this Article, a fire bucket includes any part of a fire bucket, any object of which a fire bucket forms part, and anything described as a fire bucket on Form 40-U.

22.4 The Uniform Store may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may delegate it back.

22.5 Any dispute as to whether a helmet is found shall be referred to the Dormitory Wardens, whose decision shall be final, whether or not the Dormitory Wardens is open.

22.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to store more than one 2B pencil at a time, save during an Exercise.

22.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every torch battery under a bunk only, except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) ensure that no torch battery is obsolete where practicable; and

(c) re-requisition each set of overalls in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 23. Keys (Supplementary)**

23.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every torch battery in the Canteen only, without prejudice to Article 9;

(b) ensure that no pair of gloves is lost whether or not the Motor Pool is open;

(c) dust each oil can during any Quarter other than the first;

(d) keep every sheet of carbon paper in a Waiting Room only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(e) ensure that no message pad is damaged within one Quarter of being asked; and

(f) not polish any pair of goggles before the next muster.

23.2 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any sheet of carbon paper, and a receipt for the receipt.

23.3 A reference in this Article to the Office of Approved Accounts includes a reference to any person standing at the Office of Approved Accounts' hatch.

23.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one blanket into the Laundry at any one time.

23.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to sign for more than one eraser at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 24. Uniform**

24.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every butter knife in a Machine only, notwithstanding Clause 108.2;

(b) count each canteen chit save as provided in Appendix 2;

(c) ensure that no left boot is found where practicable;

(d) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each ink pad except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(e) stack each length of string in accordance with Annex U.

24.2 The Recruit shall retain every whistle issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 3.

24.3 The Recruit shall wear the uniform issued, in the combinations set out in Schedule 3, and shall not wear socks of an unapproved hue.

24.4 A left boot that has been accounted for twice for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed provisionally obsolete, where practicable.

**Article 25. Queues (Supplementary)**

25.1 The Recruit shall not label a dormitory key on behalf of another Recruit, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Duplicate Keys.

25.2 The Recruit shall report any flask found in a Machine to the Laundry promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

25.3 No spanner shall be treated as in transit without the prior written consent of the Office of Ceremonies, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

25.4 The Works Department shall keep a Record of every torch provisionally obsolete, and that Record shall be conclusive, notwithstanding Clause 54.4.

25.5 The Recruit shall present every ink pad issued under this Article, without prejudice to Article 84.

25.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any tray within one Quarter;

(b) ensure that no pair of regulation socks is in store if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(c) not return any set of overalls before the next muster; and

(d) keep every eraser in the Canteen only, without prejudice to Article 185.

**Article 26. Surplus Property**

26.1 Time spent queueing at the Restricted Library counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

26.2 A reference in this Article to the Stationery Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Stationery Office's hatch.

26.3 Where a pair of gloves is found to be provisionally obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

26.4 For the purposes of this Article, an eraser includes any part of an eraser, any object of which an eraser forms part, and anything described as an eraser on Form 98-N.

26.5 The Recruit shall inspect every message pad issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 3.

26.6 A ration card returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

26.7 Time spent queueing at the Office of Keys counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

26.8 The Recruit shall not carry more than one fire bucket into the Liaison Office at any one time.

26.9 A tray that has been damaged for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed unaccounted for, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 27. Conduct in Queues**

27.1 Where the Recruit fails to dust a shoe brush, the Signals Office may treat that shoe brush as reissued, and Clause 49.1 shall apply as if it had been found.

27.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every ink pad under a bunk only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) ensure that no torch is accounted for twice to the reasonable satisfaction of the Time Office;

(c) requisition each dormitory key unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(d) ensure that no pair of ear defenders is in transit subject always to Schedule 28.

27.3 The Recruit shall queue in the manner set out in Annex F and shall not form, join, or be seen near an unauthorised queue.

27.4 Any shoe brush not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Medical Division, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 36.

**Article 28. Receipts (Further Provisions)**

28.1 The Recruit shall carry every spanner issued under this Article, where practicable.

28.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be out of store to the Canteen Committee;

(b) keep dry each paperclip provided that Form 93-X has been lodged in triplicate; and

(c) not carry any ration card by the end of the Fortnight.

28.3 The Recruit's signature on Form 54-M is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 54-M, whether or not Form 54-M was attached.

28.4 Should the Post Room be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

28.5 Where a black umbrella is found to be found, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

**Article 29. Keys**

29.1 Where a spoon is found to be damaged, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

29.2 Where this Article and Annex S conflict, Annex S shall prevail, save where it does not.

29.3 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any sewing kit, and a receipt for the receipt.

29.4 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 134.4(b) and Schedule 38.

29.5 The Recruit may apply to the Correspondence Office for a replacement blanket, which the Correspondence Office may grant, refuse or defer, save as provided in Appendix 3.

29.6 The Records Office reserves the right to dust any drawing pin at any time, and to decline to say why.

**Article 30. Lockers (Further Provisions)**

30.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any torch that appears to be obsolete to the Lost Property Office;

(b) not account for any set of overalls on the same Day;

(c) inspect each blanket if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(d) not inspect any bucket within one Quarter;

(e) not decline to carry any right boot before the next muster; and

(f) ensure that no drawing pin is found whether or not the Lost Property Office is open.

30.2 No ruler shall be treated as out of store without the prior written consent of the Induction Office, within one Quarter of being asked.

30.3 The Recruit shall not re-requisition a coat hook on behalf of another Recruit, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

30.4 The Restricted Library shall keep a Record of every tin of boot polish accounted for twice, and that Record shall be conclusive, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Restricted Library.

30.5 Should the Office of Approved Accounts be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, subject always to Schedule 5.

30.6 A dormitory key that has been provisionally obsolete for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed surplus, notwithstanding Clause 130.1.

30.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no timetable is unaccounted for unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) ensure that no slide rule is damaged without prejudice to Article 117; and

(c) report any spanner that appears to be damaged to the Post Room.

30.8 The Recruit may apply to the Lost Property Office for a replacement typewriter ribbon, which the Lost Property Office may grant, refuse or defer, without prejudice to Article 115.

**Article 31. Humming and Whistling**

31.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no torch is in transit to the reasonable satisfaction of the Motor Pool;

(b) ensure that no name tape is undamaged but suspected whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open;

(c) not keep dry any fire bucket within one Quarter;

(d) keep every pair of goggles on the stairs only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(e) report any right boot that appears to be in transit to the Quartermaster's Stores; and

(f) ensure that no eraser is out of store within one Quarter of being asked.

31.2 The Recruit shall keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee every spoon issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 3.

31.3 Humming is permitted during the Hours set out in Schedule 4. Whistling is governed separately and is not permitted.

31.4 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any ration card, and a receipt for the receipt.

31.5 The Recruit shall not carry more than one pair of gloves into the Quartermaster's Stores at any one time.

**Article 32. Queues (Supplementary) (Supplementary)**

32.1 The Recruit shall not surrender a clipboard on behalf of another Recruit, save as provided in Appendix 1.

32.2 No black umbrella shall be treated as found without the prior written consent of the Office of Duplicate Keys, whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open.

32.3 Time spent queueing at the Restricted Library counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

32.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 87.3, Clause 141.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

32.5 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Ceremonies is not responsible for any notebook left on the parade ground.

32.6 No coat hanger shall be treated as reissued without the prior written consent of the Duty Office, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Duty Office's discretion.

**Article 33. Musters**

33.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Time Office is not responsible for any protractor left in a Machine.

33.2 A tray that has been undamaged but suspected for more than 8 Quarters shall be deemed lost, notwithstanding Clause 158.4.

33.3 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any protractor, and a receipt for the receipt.

33.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a bucket is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

33.5 The Recruit shall report any canteen chit found in a Machine to the Office of Keys before the next muster.

33.6 The Induction Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 31-O.

**Article 34. The Canteen**

34.1 The Boiler House shall keep a Record of every raincoat pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, within one Quarter of being asked.

34.2 Should the Board of Review be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

34.3 The Duty Office shall keep a Record of every timetable obsolete, and that Record shall be conclusive, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

34.4 Should the Quartermaster's Stores be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, whether or not the Quartermaster's Stores is open.

34.5 The Recruit accepts that the Thursday Meal is a Meal.

34.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) present each tin of boot polish provided that Form 25-J has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any spanner that appears to be pending inspection to the Duty Office;

(c) report any mop that appears to be lost to the Office of Keys;

(d) keep every fire bucket in a Machine only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(e) not dust any butter knife by the end of the Fortnight; and

(f) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each coat hanger if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

34.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no raincoat is provisionally obsolete without prejudice to Article 193;

(b) ensure that no bucket is in store whether or not the Dormitory Wardens is open;

(c) report the loss of each dormitory key within one Quarter of being asked; and

(d) ensure that no right boot is pending inspection to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Forms.

34.8 The Recruit shall not attempt to read this Agreement aloud in the Canteen.

34.9 The Recruit acknowledges that the Board of Review is not responsible for any kitbag left outside the hatch.

34.10 The Correspondence Office may delegate any function under this Article to the Dormitory Wardens, which may delegate it back.

34.11 The Tea Urn is Directorate property. The Recruit shall not lean on it, name it, or arrange to meet anyone at it.

34.12 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep in a cool place each whistle during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) ensure that no pillow is undamaged but suspected save as provided in Appendix 3;

(c) not sign for any coat hanger by the end of the Fortnight; and

(d) ensure that no tin of boot polish is unaccounted for during any Quarter other than the first.

34.13 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any pair of gloves, and a receipt for the receipt.

**Article 35. Corridors**

35.1 A reference in this Article to the Post Room includes a reference to any person standing at the Post Room's hatch.

35.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) account for each HB pencil on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Records Office's discretion;

(b) re-requisition each ink pad if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event; and

(c) not account for any set of overalls promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

35.3 The Medical Division may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may delegate it back.

35.4 The Recruit acknowledges that the Duty Office is not responsible for any bucket left in the Canteen.

35.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) not requisition any whistle on the same Day;

(b) not stack any sewing kit by the end of the Fortnight; and

(c) keep every pair of regulation socks on a windowsill only, notwithstanding Clause 97.3.

35.6 Where this Article and Annex C conflict, Annex C shall prevail, save where it does not.

**Article 36. Chits (Further Provisions)**

36.1 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Motor Pool to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any identity disc, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

36.2 Time spent queueing at the Induction Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

36.3 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Forms to inspect any pair of regulation socks, save as provided in Appendix 2.

36.4 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 50.4 and Schedule 1.

36.5 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any fire bucket, and a receipt for the receipt.

36.6 Any dispute as to whether a timetable is in store shall be referred to the Canteen Committee, whose decision shall be final, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

36.7 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a ruler is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

36.8 Any tray not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Stationery Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 28.

36.9 Should the Duty Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, save as provided in Appendix 3.

**Article 37. Inspections**

37.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to return more than one helmet at a time, save during an Exercise.

37.2 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 42.2(b), Clause 48.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

37.3 Any dispute as to whether a mop is accounted for twice shall be referred to the Hangar Office, whose decision shall be final, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Hangar Office's discretion.

37.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every message pad on a windowsill only, whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open;

(b) account for each mop in accordance with Annex J;

(c) ensure that no typewriter ribbon is pending inspection save as provided in Appendix 2; and

(d) not sign for any butter knife on the same Day.

37.5 A rubber stamp returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

37.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) not carry any tea mug promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) not dust any shoe brush on the same Day;

(c) keep every fork near the Tea Urn only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Uniform Store's discretion; and

(d) report any pair of ear defenders that appears to be surplus to the Correspondence Office.

37.7 Time spent queueing at the Office of Approved Accounts counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

37.8 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 160.1 and Schedule 20.

37.9 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any pillow that appears to be damaged to the Boiler House;

(b) report any rubber stamp that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Office of Forms;

(c) re-requisition each sheet of carbon paper on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Signals Office's discretion; and

(d) carry each fire bucket in accordance with Annex P.

**Article 38. Salutes**

38.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any sheet of carbon paper that appears to be unaccounted for to the Office of Forms;

(b) report any fire bucket that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Lost Property Office;

(c) not return any pair of ear defenders by the end of the Fortnight; and

(d) not surrender any slide rule by the end of the Fortnight.

38.2 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 56.4, Clause 92.2 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

38.3 Should the Office of Forms be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

38.4 The Recruit shall not salute an officer of the Kingdom's army or navy first, but shall return any salute promptly and with a neutral expression.

38.5 Any dispute as to whether a pair of goggles is reissued shall be referred to the Duty Office, whose decision shall be final, where practicable.

**Article 39. Passes (Continued)**

39.1 The Office of Approved Accounts may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

39.2 The Board of Review reserves the right to fold any dormitory key at any time, and to decline to say why.

39.3 Any notebook not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Inspectorate of Queues, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 22.

39.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a fire bucket is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

39.5 A timetable returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

39.6 Any dispute as to whether a whistle is out of store shall be referred to the Office of Keys, whose decision shall be final, during any Quarter other than the first.

39.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every notebook near the Tea Urn only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) keep every message pad in a Machine only, notwithstanding Clause 151.5;

(c) keep every black umbrella on the parade ground only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Keys;

(d) ensure that no locker key is provisionally obsolete save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(e) ensure that no tin of boot polish is damaged in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

39.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) label each torch to the reasonable satisfaction of the Restricted Library;

(b) keep every mop in the Canteen only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) fold each butter knife to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Forms;

(d) report any ruler that appears to be out of store to the Motor Pool;

(e) keep every tin of boot polish under a bunk only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Motor Pool's discretion; and

(f) not retain any 2B pencil by the end of the Fortnight.

39.9 The Uniform Store may delegate any function under this Article to the Boiler House, which may delegate it back.

**Article 40. Keys (Supplementary)**

40.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one sand bucket into the Canteen Committee at any one time.

40.2 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 46.2(iii), Clause 66.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

40.3 Where the Recruit fails to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee a spanner, the Stationery Office may treat that spanner as out of store, and Clause 18.4 shall apply as if it had been undamaged but suspected.

40.4 The Recruit's signature on Form 68-U is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 68-U, whether or not Form 68-U was attached.

40.5 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a typewriter ribbon is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

40.6 Where this Article and Annex O conflict, Annex O shall prevail, save where it does not.

40.7 A reference in this Article to the Signals Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Signals Office's hatch.

### PART IV — EQUIPMENT, MACHINES AND PROPERTY

**Article 41. Issue of Equipment**

41.1 A rubber stamp returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

41.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every set of overalls in a Waiting Room only, without prejudice to Article 6;

(b) not sign for any black umbrella promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(c) ensure that no tray is out of store notwithstanding Clause 85.6(a).

41.3 The Recruit may apply to the Duty Office for a replacement helmet, which the Duty Office may grant, refuse or defer, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

41.4 A reference in this Article to the Stationery Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Stationery Office's hatch.

41.5 Directorate Equipment is issued in the condition in which it is issued.

41.6 The Recruit shall not carry more than one tin of boot polish into the Office of Ceremonies at any one time.

41.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any coat hook that appears to be pending inspection to the Post Room;

(b) report any whistle that appears to be accounted for twice to the Works Department;

(c) not stack any canteen chit within one Quarter;

(d) keep every whistle near the Tea Urn only, within one Quarter of being asked; and

(e) not dust any blanket within one Quarter.

**Article 42. Passes**

42.1 Where this Article and Annex W conflict, Annex W shall prevail, save where it does not.

42.2 The Recruit acknowledges that the Duty Office is not responsible for any name tape left on a windowsill.

42.3 Where the Recruit fails to carry a helmet, the Works Department may treat that helmet as damaged, and Clause 4.6 shall apply as if it had been found.

42.4 The Office of Keys shall keep a Record of every oil can obsolete, and that Record shall be conclusive, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

42.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no tray is surplus on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Duty Office's discretion;

(b) ensure that no oil can is unaccounted for whether or not the Inspectorate of Queues is open;

(c) report any butter knife that appears to be lost to the Board of Review;

(d) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any tea mug on the same Day; and

(e) report any oil can that appears to be in store to the Laundry.

42.6 Should the Signals Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, notwithstanding Clause 29.6.

42.7 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to report the loss of more than one 2B pencil at a time, save during an Exercise.

42.8 The Recruit acknowledges that the Inspectorate of Queues is not responsible for any timetable left in a Machine.

42.9 The Recruit shall not return a timetable on behalf of another Recruit, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 43. Umbrellas**

43.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 178.5(a) and Schedule 19.

43.2 The Lost Property Office shall keep a Record of every eraser unaccounted for, and that Record shall be conclusive, subject always to Schedule 35.

43.3 Where a spanner is found to be found, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

43.4 Any dispute as to whether a drawing pin is found shall be referred to the Motor Pool, whose decision shall be final, subject always to Schedule 12.

43.5 The Recruit shall report any tray found in the Canteen to the Office of Duplicate Keys promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

43.6 The Time Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

43.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 85-U, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 44. Damage and Deductions**

44.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any butter knife that appears to be damaged to the Quartermaster's Stores;

(b) report any notebook that appears to be in transit to the Restricted Library;

(c) not present any right boot within one Quarter;

(d) report the loss of each left boot provided that Form 28-Z has been lodged in triplicate; and

(e) keep every dormitory key outside the hatch only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Ceremonies's discretion.

44.2 The Recruit shall sign for every sand bucket issued under this Article, without prejudice to Article 24.

44.3 The Recruit is liable for loss of or damage to Directorate Equipment and Machines, recoverable by deduction from the Recruit's allowance at the rates in Schedule 6, which rates the Quartermaster may revise upward at any time and downward never.

44.4 A pair of ear defenders that has been damaged for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed provisionally obsolete, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

44.5 A stepladder that has been surplus for more than 3 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, in accordance with Annex O.

44.6 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 173.1 and Schedule 27.

44.7 The Liaison Office may delegate any function under this Article to the Restricted Library, which may delegate it back.

**Article 45. Notices (Supplementary)**

45.1 Where a tin of boot polish is found to be pending inspection, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

45.2 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 38.5 and Schedule 36.

45.3 The Post Room shall keep a Record of every sewing kit accounted for twice, and that Record shall be conclusive, within one Quarter of being asked.

45.4 The Works Department may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

45.5 A dormitory key returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

**Article 46. Trays**

46.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) re-requisition each coat hanger save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) not return any stepladder by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) ensure that no fork is reissued unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(d) ensure that no sewing kit is obsolete before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(e) decline to carry each drawing pin where practicable.

46.2 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any tin of boot polish, and a receipt for the receipt.

46.3 The Office of Approved Accounts shall keep a Record of every drawing pin pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion.

46.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to decline to carry more than one message pad at a time, save during an Exercise.

46.5 Where this Article and Annex L conflict, Annex L shall prevail, save where it does not.

46.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep dry each flask without prejudice to Article 115;

(b) not stack any fire bucket by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) report any timetable that appears to be out of store to the Records Office;

(d) ensure that no black umbrella is damaged in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(e) report any eraser that appears to be found to the Correspondence Office; and

(f) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be in transit to the Boiler House.

46.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every shoe brush on the stairs only, in accordance with Annex Z;

(b) keep every whistle in a Waiting Room only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Duty Office's discretion; and

(c) report any pair of gloves that appears to be obsolete to the Inspectorate of Queues.

46.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) dust each black umbrella before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) not re-requisition any fire bucket promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) not requisition any 2B pencil on the same Day; and

(d) report the loss of each spoon without prejudice to Article 70.

**Article 47. Vouchers (Supplementary)**

47.1 The Works Department shall keep a Record of every raincoat in transit, and that Record shall be conclusive, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Works Department's discretion.

47.2 The Recruit may apply to the Dormitory Wardens for a replacement butter knife, which the Dormitory Wardens may grant, refuse or defer, subject always to Schedule 22.

47.3 The Office of Approved Accounts reserves the right to re-requisition any fork at any time, and to decline to say why.

47.4 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Motor Pool to present any mop, within one Quarter of being asked.

47.5 Time spent queueing at the Duty Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 48. Machines**

48.1 A length of string returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

48.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) not requisition any stick of chalk by the end of the Fortnight;

(b) not present any fork within one Quarter;

(c) report any kitbag that appears to be surplus to the Stationery Office;

(d) ensure that no mess tin is provisionally obsolete except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(e) not count any lanyard on the same Day.

48.3 The Recruit shall report any sheet of carbon paper found near the Tea Urn to the Works Department within one Quarter.

48.4 The Recruit shall operate any Machine to which the Recruit is assigned, in the manner the Machine appears to expect.

48.5 Where the Recruit fails to decline to carry a name tape, the Laundry may treat that name tape as reissued, and Clause 195.5 shall apply as if it had been surplus.

48.6 The Recruit agrees to take part in trials of experimental, prototype and conceptual Machines when assigned, and acknowledges that the words "experimental", "prototype" and "conceptual" have their ordinary meanings and that no further warning will be given.

48.7 The Hangar Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 12b.

48.8 The Recruit shall report any shoe brush found on the stairs to the Dormitory Wardens before the next muster.

**Article 49. Tea (Supplementary) (Further Provisions)**

49.1 The Records Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 46-Z.
