49.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Liaison Office to carry any helmet, where practicable.

49.3 The Recruit shall report any eraser found outside the hatch to the Boiler House promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

49.4 The Laundry may delegate any function under this Article to the Medical Division, which may delegate it back.

49.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every lanyard in a corridor only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Records Office's discretion;

(b) ensure that no identity disc is surplus during any Quarter other than the first;

(c) ensure that no copy of this Agreement is provisionally obsolete before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(d) not decline to carry any sewing kit by the end of the Fortnight.

49.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every slide rule in a Machine only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Inspectorate of Queues;

(b) ensure that no ration card is obsolete provided that Form 89-G has been lodged in triplicate;

(c) report any tea mug that appears to be accounted for twice to the Office of Keys;

(d) not present any tray promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(e) ensure that no eraser is pending inspection on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Boiler House's discretion; and

(f) report any tray that appears to be accounted for twice to the Motor Pool.

49.7 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Forms for a replacement rag, which the Office of Forms may grant, refuse or defer, whether or not the Office of Forms is open.

49.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no typewriter ribbon is out of store whether or not the Restricted Library is open;

(b) not report the loss of any spanner within one Quarter;

(c) keep every typewriter ribbon in a Machine only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(d) keep every clipboard on a windowsill only, where practicable;

(e) report any HB pencil that appears to be in transit to the Lost Property Office; and

(f) keep every ration card on the stairs only, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

**Article 50. Vouchers (Continued)**

50.1 The Inspectorate of Queues may, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails, require the Recruit to polish any spoon.

50.2 A rubber stamp returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

50.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 28.5, Clause 119.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

50.4 The Recruit shall present every helmet issued under this Article, except on alternate Tuesdays.

50.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to account for more than one whistle at a time, save during an Exercise.

50.6 Where a canteen chit is found to be surplus, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

**Article 51. Naming of Machines**

51.1 The Boiler House may, in accordance with Annex T, require the Recruit to stack any sewing kit.

51.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) not account for any dormitory key within one Quarter;

(b) ensure that no black umbrella is accounted for twice where practicable;

(c) report any identity disc that appears to be found to the Hangar Office; and

(d) keep every pair of regulation socks on the stairs only, within one Quarter of being asked.

51.3 The Recruit shall not name any Machine after a member of staff, living or retired, or after the Quartermaster in particular.

51.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no right boot is surplus where practicable;

(b) not re-requisition any dormitory key within one Quarter;

(c) keep every identity disc outside the hatch only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(d) report any whistle that appears to be out of store to the Uniform Store; and

(e) surrender each HB pencil in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

51.5 The Recruit shall polish every rag issued under this Article, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Motor Pool's discretion.

51.6 The Recruit shall retain every spanner issued under this Article, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 52. Surplus Property (Further Provisions)**

52.1 No spoon shall be treated as surplus without the prior written consent of the Correspondence Office, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

52.2 For the purposes of this Article, a timetable includes any part of a timetable, any object of which a timetable forms part, and anything described as a timetable on Form 56-A.

52.3 The Recruit's signature on Form 55-T is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 55-T, whether or not Form 55-T was attached.

52.4 An HB pencil that has been obsolete for more than 6 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

52.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) account for each torch whether or not the Dormitory Wardens is open;

(b) return each sheet of carbon paper provided that Form 48-F has been lodged in triplicate;

(c) report any spanner that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Time Office;

(d) not dust any paperclip within one Quarter; and

(e) not decline to carry any message pad before the next muster.

52.6 The Recruit shall stack every whistle issued under this Article, whether or not the Office of Ceremonies is open.

52.7 The Recruit's signature on Form 2-N is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 2-N, whether or not Form 2-N was attached.

52.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no flask is out of store notwithstanding Clause 65.5;

(b) not carry any typewriter ribbon on the same Day; and

(c) keep every set of overalls outside the hatch only, whether or not the Office of Keys is open.

52.9 Where a stick of chalk is found to be in transit, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

**Article 53. Carbon Paper**

53.1 The Recruit shall report any canteen chit found in the Canteen to the Office of Keys by the end of the Fortnight.

53.2 For the purposes of this Article, a flask includes any part of a flask, any object of which a flask forms part, and anything described as a flask on Form 39-O.

53.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 41.6(b), Clause 24.5 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

53.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a fork is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

53.5 Where an ink pad is found to be surplus, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

**Article 54. Found Property**

54.1 Where the Recruit fails to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee a torch battery, the Hangar Office may treat that torch battery as surplus, and Clause 188.4 shall apply as if it had been pending inspection.

54.2 The Recruit may apply to the Inspectorate of Queues for a replacement drawing pin, which the Inspectorate of Queues may grant, refuse or defer, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

54.3 Any object, material, substance or residue recovered, found, handled, carried or trodden in by the Recruit in the course of any Duty becomes Directorate property on the instant, including any object the Recruit did not know they had, and the Recruit shall surrender it on request.

54.4 Any dispute as to whether an eraser is surplus shall be referred to the Duty Office, whose decision shall be final, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

54.5 The Records Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

54.6 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Restricted Library to present any eraser, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 55. Requisitions (Further Provisions)**

55.1 Where this Article and Annex S conflict, Annex S shall prevail, save where it does not.

55.2 A pair of ear defenders that has been reissued for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed damaged, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

55.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 57.4(e)(ii) and Schedule 11.

55.4 The Office of Keys reserves the right to retain any protractor at any time, and to decline to say why.

55.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 135.2, Clause 185.5(a) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

55.6 Where a fork is found to be lost, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

55.7 Any shoe brush not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 8.

**Article 56. Receipts**

56.1 The Office of Keys may delegate any function under this Article to the Correspondence Office, which may delegate it back.

56.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every protractor on a windowsill only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) keep every spoon on the parade ground only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(c) report any shoe brush that appears to be surplus to the Induction Office; and

(d) decline to carry each 2B pencil except on alternate Tuesdays.

56.3 The Recruit shall report the loss of every left boot issued under this Article, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Canteen Committee's discretion.

56.4 The Liaison Office shall keep a Record of every butter knife in transit, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

56.5 For the purposes of this Article, a ration card includes any part of a ration card, any object of which a ration card forms part, and anything described as a ration card on Form 1-B(ii).

56.6 The Recruit shall not retain a tea mug on behalf of another Recruit, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Hangar Office.

**Article 57. Socks (Further Provisions)**

57.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 86.4 and Schedule 8.

57.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no blanket is unaccounted for whether or not the Lost Property Office is open;

(b) re-requisition each coat hook subject always to Schedule 16;

(c) keep every coat hanger on the parade ground only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(d) ensure that no oil can is lost provided that Form 75-W has been lodged in triplicate.

57.3 Time spent queueing at the Liaison Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

57.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no locker key is provisionally obsolete provided that Form 54-T has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any clipboard that appears to be out of store to the Induction Office;

(c) report any ration card that appears to be out of store to the Induction Office;

(d) keep every set of overalls in a corridor only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(e) keep every shoe brush on the parade ground only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(f) keep every identity disc in a corridor only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Forms's discretion.

57.5 Any name tape not collected before the next muster shall pass to the Canteen Committee, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 6.

57.6 The Recruit shall not carry more than one paperclip into the Correspondence Office at any one time.

57.7 The Stationery Office reserves the right to count any fire bucket at any time, and to decline to say why.

57.8 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to sign for more than one spoon at a time, save during an Exercise.

57.9 The Office of Approved Accounts may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

**Article 58. Sealed Envelopes**

58.1 Any flask not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Office of Ceremonies, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 25.

58.2 The Induction Office shall keep a Record of every notebook out of store, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

58.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every paperclip near the Tea Urn only, where practicable;

(b) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be in transit to the Lost Property Office; and

(c) report any lanyard that appears to be damaged to the Records Office.

58.4 The Recruit shall keep, and shall not open, any sealed envelope issued to the Recruit under this Agreement until instructed to open it, and agrees that the instruction may itself arrive in a sealed envelope.

58.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) not sign for any kitbag promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) report any coat hook that appears to be out of store to the Uniform Store;

(c) not polish any shoe brush before the next muster; and

(d) ensure that no pair of regulation socks is reissued without prejudice to Article 180.

58.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any right boot that appears to be damaged to the Office of Ceremonies;

(b) not stack any HB pencil before the next muster;

(c) keep in a cool place each raincoat in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(d) ensure that no clipboard is pending inspection within one Quarter of being asked.

58.7 The Recruit shall report the loss of every 2B pencil issued under this Article, subject always to Schedule 30.

58.8 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Boiler House to fold any slide rule, save as provided in Appendix 3.

**Article 59. Umbrellas (Further Provisions)**

59.1 Should the Time Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, during any Quarter other than the first.

59.2 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any helmet, and a receipt for the receipt.

59.3 The Recruit shall not carry more than one protractor into the Board of Review at any one time.

59.4 Where a black umbrella is found to be pending inspection, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

59.5 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 186.5(f) and Schedule 20.

**Article 60. Ledgers (Continued)**

60.1 The Board of Review may, except on alternate Tuesdays, require the Recruit to return any flask.

60.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every set of overalls in a Waiting Room only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) account for each stepladder in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) report any notebook that appears to be damaged to the Office of Ceremonies;

(d) report any torch battery that appears to be surplus to the Stationery Office;

(e) report any blanket that appears to be surplus to the Medical Division; and

(f) ensure that no notebook is provisionally obsolete provided that Form 1-A has been lodged in triplicate.

60.3 Time spent queueing at the Post Room counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

60.4 A name tape that has been found for more than 8 Quarters shall be deemed in transit, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

60.5 The Canteen Committee shall keep a Record of every stick of chalk found, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

60.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a dormitory key is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

60.7 The Recruit may apply to the Signals Office for a replacement drawing pin, which the Signals Office may grant, refuse or defer, where practicable.

**Article 61. Requisitions (Further Provisions)**

61.1 A reference in this Article to the Lost Property Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Lost Property Office's hatch.

61.2 The Recruit may apply to the Duty Office for a replacement notebook, which the Duty Office may grant, refuse or defer, where practicable.

61.3 Where this Article and Annex C conflict, Annex C shall prevail, save where it does not.

61.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to present more than one dormitory key at a time, save during an Exercise.

61.5 The Hangar Office may, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Hangar Office, require the Recruit to surrender any coat hook.

61.6 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any butter knife, and a receipt for the receipt.

**Article 62. Custody**

62.1 Time spent queueing at the Office of Approved Accounts counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

62.2 The Recruit shall not carry more than one set of overalls into the Motor Pool at any one time.

62.3 Any dispute as to whether a sewing kit is damaged shall be referred to the Hangar Office, whose decision shall be final, whether or not the Hangar Office is open.

62.4 The Recruit accepts custody of such items as may be entrusted to the Recruit from time to time, including items the Recruit is not told have been entrusted to them, and is responsible for their safe keeping.

62.5 The Recruit shall return every fork issued under this Article, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

62.6 Where a mess tin is found to be damaged, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

62.7 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 56.4(d)(i), Clause 38.4(f) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

**Article 63. Duplicates (Further Provisions)**

63.1 The Recruit's signature on Form 73-B is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 73-B, whether or not Form 73-B was attached.

63.2 The Recruit acknowledges that the Records Office is not responsible for any mop left near the Tea Urn.

63.3 The Hangar Office shall keep a Record of every pair of goggles lost, and that Record shall be conclusive, without prejudice to Article 36.

63.4 The Medical Division may delegate any function under this Article to the Duty Office, which may delegate it back.

63.5 A reference in this Article to the Boiler House includes a reference to any person standing at the Boiler House's hatch.

63.6 A rubber stamp that has been obsolete for more than 2 Quarters shall be deemed lost, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Ceremonies's discretion.

63.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every fork on a windowsill only, where practicable;

(b) keep every spanner in the Canteen only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(c) report any eraser that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Boiler House;

(d) decline to carry each stepladder unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(e) requisition each pair of regulation socks provided that Form 40-U has been lodged in triplicate.

**Article 64. Queues (Supplementary)**

64.1 The Recruit shall report any slide rule found in a Machine to the Correspondence Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

64.2 Where a 2B pencil is found to be obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

64.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 60.5 and Schedule 32.

64.4 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 120.4 and Schedule 35.

64.5 The Restricted Library may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

64.6 The Uniform Store need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 41-Z.

### PART V — COMMUNICATIONS AND RECORDS

**Article 65. Directorate Communications Equipment**

65.1 The Canteen Committee may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

65.2 A length of string returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

65.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every sheet of carbon paper on the parade ground only, provided that Form 29-D has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any blanket that appears to be in store to the Restricted Library; and

(c) not inspect any slide rule promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

65.4 For the purposes of this Article, a ration card includes any part of a ration card, any object of which a ration card forms part, and anything described as a ration card on Form 88-X.

65.5 Any telephone, terminal, radio, notebook, noticeboard or message pad supplied by the Directorate is Directorate Equipment for all purposes, including this Part.

65.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a spanner is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

65.7 The Recruit shall not carry more than one torch into the Liaison Office at any one time.

**Article 66. Receipts (Continued)**

66.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mop into the Correspondence Office at any one time.

66.2 The Canteen Committee reserves the right to carry any torch at any time, and to decline to say why.

66.3 Time spent queueing at the Inspectorate of Queues counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

66.4 Where a black umbrella is found to be unaccounted for, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

66.5 The Recruit may apply to the Liaison Office for a replacement blanket, which the Liaison Office may grant, refuse or defer, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

66.6 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 23.5(f) and Schedule 32.

66.7 Where a 2B pencil is found to be pending inspection, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

66.8 The Boiler House shall keep a Record of every flask reissued, and that Record shall be conclusive, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

**Article 67. Corridors**

67.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether an oil can is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

67.2 Time spent queueing at the Correspondence Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

67.3 The Induction Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

67.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not fold any left boot within one Quarter;

(b) keep every kitbag in a Machine only, without prejudice to Article 41;

(c) report any name tape that appears to be surplus to the Office of Ceremonies; and

(d) report any tray that appears to be in store to the Dormitory Wardens.

67.5 The Signals Office shall keep a Record of every stepladder reissued, and that Record shall be conclusive, save as provided in Appendix 3.

67.6 Where the Recruit fails to carry an identity disc, the Office of Keys may treat that identity disc as obsolete, and Clause 37.4(b) shall apply as if it had been unaccounted for.

67.7 Where the Recruit fails to dust a lanyard, the Board of Review may treat that lanyard as in store, and Clause 196.2 shall apply as if it had been reissued.

67.8 The Inspectorate of Queues may delegate any function under this Article to the Stationery Office, which may delegate it back.

67.9 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 2.5 and Schedule 12.

**Article 68. Correspondence**

68.1 An eraser that has been accounted for twice for more than 3 Quarters shall be deemed found, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

68.2 Where a raincoat is found to be pending inspection, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

68.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every clipboard on a windowsill only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) keep every sheet of carbon paper near the Tea Urn only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) keep every locker key in the Canteen only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(d) ensure that no black umbrella is reissued before the Tea Urn is emptied.

68.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) inspect each pair of gloves to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board of Review;

(b) report any rag that appears to be in store to the Office of Approved Accounts;

(c) ensure that no flask is damaged save as provided in Appendix 3; and

(d) not carry any tin of boot polish on the same Day.

68.5 The Recruit consents to the Directorate reading, copying, keeping and acting upon any correspondence, message, note, drawing or record made, sent, received, begun, abandoned or left lying about by the Recruit by means of Directorate Equipment, at any time, for any purpose the Directorate considers proper, without further notice, and agrees that such consent extends to Form 12b and to any form that replaces it.

68.6 A 2B pencil returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

68.7 The Office of Duplicate Keys need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 56-A.

68.8 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to return more than one shoe brush at a time, save during an Exercise.

68.9 The Office of Forms shall keep a Record of every protractor unaccounted for, and that Record shall be conclusive, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

68.10 The Recruit waives any objection to the matters in this Article, including objections the Recruit has not yet thought of.

68.11 Where a raincoat is found to be in transit, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

68.12 Where a coat hanger is found to be reissued, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

68.13 Where the Recruit fails to store a blanket, the Office of Approved Accounts may treat that blanket as pending inspection, and Clause 25.4(f) shall apply as if it had been lost.

**Article 69. Waiting Rooms (Continued)**

69.1 Where the Recruit fails to dust a canteen chit, the Stationery Office may treat that canteen chit as out of store, and Clause 144.4 shall apply as if it had been damaged.

69.2 The Uniform Store shall keep a Record of every sewing kit lost, and that Record shall be conclusive, whether or not the Uniform Store is open.

69.3 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any helmet, and a receipt for the receipt.

69.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every rag in the Canteen only, whether or not the Restricted Library is open;

(b) not dust any protractor before the next muster;

(c) not surrender any tray promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) ensure that no locker key is accounted for twice notwithstanding Clause 134.4.

69.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) not requisition any pair of goggles promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) not keep dry any torch promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) report any HB pencil that appears to be accounted for twice to the Lost Property Office; and

(d) keep every clipboard under a bunk only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

69.6 Time spent queueing at the Stationery Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 70. Notices**

70.1 The Office of Approved Accounts need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 12-K.

70.2 Any dispute as to whether a tin of boot polish is out of store shall be referred to the Records Office, whose decision shall be final, in accordance with Annex L.

70.3 Any dispute as to whether a torch is surplus shall be referred to the Uniform Store, whose decision shall be final, whether or not the Uniform Store is open.

70.4 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any coat hook, and a receipt for the receipt.

70.5 The Recruit shall not fold a rag on behalf of another Recruit, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

70.6 Any tin of boot polish not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Signals Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 31.

**Article 71. Seals (Further Provisions)**

71.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 115.5 and Schedule 15.

71.2 The Works Department reserves the right to count any pillow at any time, and to decline to say why.

71.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any locker key that appears to be unaccounted for to the Motor Pool;

(b) not requisition any sewing kit before the next muster; and

(c) account for each kitbag before the Tea Urn is emptied.

71.4 The Inspectorate of Queues may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

71.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) sign for each canteen chit subject always to Schedule 14;

(b) keep every mess tin on the stairs only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(c) not carry any paperclip by the end of the Fortnight;

(d) not store any copy of this Agreement before the next muster;

(e) keep every black umbrella on a windowsill only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event; and

(f) stack each clipboard on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Signals Office's discretion.

71.6 The Recruit shall report any kitbag found under a bunk to the Hangar Office before the next muster.

**Article 72. Records**

72.1 Any dispute as to whether a 2B pencil is undamaged but suspected shall be referred to the Office of Ceremonies, whose decision shall be final, save as provided in Appendix 3.

72.2 Any dispute as to whether a stick of chalk is damaged shall be referred to the Board of Review, whose decision shall be final, save as provided in Appendix 2.

72.3 No stepladder shall be treated as pending inspection without the prior written consent of the Duty Office, during any Quarter other than the first.

72.4 The Office of Forms may, provided that Form 7-V has been lodged in triplicate, require the Recruit to requisition any protractor.

72.5 A Record is conclusive as to its contents, and where a Record and an event disagree, the Record shall prevail.

72.6 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 171.3, Clause 196.6(c) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

72.7 Any dispute as to whether a mop is surplus shall be referred to the Stationery Office, whose decision shall be final, during any Quarter other than the first.

72.8 Where the Recruit fails to polish a rubber stamp, the Boiler House may treat that rubber stamp as reissued, and Clause 158.2(i) shall apply as if it had been unaccounted for.

**Article 73. Rotas (Continued)**

73.1 The Office of Ceremonies may delegate any function under this Article to the Board of Review, which may delegate it back.

73.2 The Recruit may apply to the Duty Office for a replacement flask, which the Duty Office may grant, refuse or defer, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Duty Office.

73.3 Any dispute as to whether a black umbrella is unaccounted for shall be referred to the Medical Division, whose decision shall be final, without prejudice to Article 104.

73.4 The Induction Office may, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails, require the Recruit to inspect any paperclip.

73.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no kitbag is reissued within one Quarter of being asked;

(b) report any whistle that appears to be reissued to the Office of Ceremonies;

(c) not return any pillow by the end of the Fortnight; and

(d) ensure that no dormitory key is damaged subject always to Schedule 35.

73.6 The Canteen Committee may delegate any function under this Article to the Stationery Office, which may delegate it back.

73.7 Time spent queueing at the Records Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 74. Ledgers (Supplementary)**

74.1 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 12-O, without prejudice to Article 131.

74.2 A reference in this Article to the Post Room includes a reference to any person standing at the Post Room's hatch.

74.3 For the purposes of this Article, a blanket includes any part of a blanket, any object of which a blanket forms part, and anything described as a blanket on Form 48-R.

74.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any clipboard that appears to be obsolete to the Induction Office;

(b) keep every clipboard on the stairs only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(c) keep every name tape under a bunk only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

74.5 The Office of Ceremonies may delegate any function under this Article to the Records Office, which may delegate it back.

74.6 A timetable returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

74.7 A length of string that has been surplus for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, except on alternate Tuesdays.

74.8 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Correspondence Office to inspect any butter knife, in accordance with Annex U.

**Article 75. Precedence (Supplementary)**

75.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any shoe brush promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) keep every torch battery in the Canteen only, save as provided in Appendix 3;

(c) return each black umbrella before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) label each right boot if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(e) keep every eraser under a bunk only, in accordance with Annex R; and

(f) present each torch battery unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

75.2 The Recruit shall label every notebook issued under this Article, during any Quarter other than the first.

75.3 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mess tin into the Uniform Store at any one time.

75.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to requisition more than one whistle at a time, save during an Exercise.

75.5 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 162.3 and Schedule 29.

75.6 The Recruit's signature on Form 54-M is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 54-M, whether or not Form 54-M was attached.

**Article 76. Approved Accounts**

76.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one sheet of carbon paper into the Lost Property Office at any one time.

76.2 No mop shall be treated as in transit without the prior written consent of the Dormitory Wardens, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Dormitory Wardens's discretion.

76.3 The Recruit shall not carry more than one rubber stamp into the Time Office at any one time.

76.4 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Approved Accounts for a replacement mess tin, which the Office of Approved Accounts may grant, refuse or defer, provided that Form 69-O has been lodged in triplicate.

76.5 The Recruit agrees to be debriefed, and re-debriefed, and to accept the Approved Account of any event as the account of that event, and shall not maintain, repeat or write down any other account once the Approved Account has been issued.

76.6 The Duty Office may, provided that Form 86-H has been lodged in triplicate, require the Recruit to carry any tin of boot polish.

**Article 77. Keys (Continued)**

77.1 The Correspondence Office shall keep a Record of every dormitory key in store, and that Record shall be conclusive, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

77.2 A reference in this Article to the Duty Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Duty Office's hatch.

77.3 Where the Recruit fails to dust a copy of this Agreement, the Post Room may treat that copy of this Agreement as pending inspection, and Clause 95.4(b) shall apply as if it had been surplus.

77.4 The Induction Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

77.5 Where a spanner is found to be surplus, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

77.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) polish each sand bucket to the reasonable satisfaction of the Medical Division;

(b) ensure that no spanner is accounted for twice during any Quarter other than the first;

(c) report any torch that appears to be lost to the Duty Office;

(d) not inspect any locker key promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(e) ensure that no tea mug is reissued to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Forms; and

(f) report any right boot that appears to be in transit to the Office of Ceremonies.

77.7 No mess tin shall be treated as damaged without the prior written consent of the Induction Office, whether or not the Induction Office is open.

**Article 78. Receipts**

78.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no sheet of carbon paper is provisionally obsolete before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) not count any rag before the next muster;

(c) not return any helmet on the same Day; and

(d) report any spanner that appears to be pending inspection to the Uniform Store.

78.2 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 149.5, Clause 59.2(iii) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

78.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no coat hanger is reissued subject always to Schedule 34;

(b) report any torch battery that appears to be pending inspection to the Hangar Office;

(c) keep every typewriter ribbon outside the hatch only, save as provided in Appendix 2;

(d) report any drawing pin that appears to be unaccounted for to the Board of Review; and

(e) keep every notebook in a corridor only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

78.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every set of overalls outside the hatch only, where practicable;

(b) stack each slide rule in accordance with Annex R; and

(c) ensure that no canteen chit is surplus unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

78.5 The Recruit may apply to the Medical Division for a replacement rag, which the Medical Division may grant, refuse or defer, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Medical Division.

**Article 79. Forgetting**

79.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a length of string is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

79.2 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a name tape is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

79.3 Where the Recruit becomes aware of anything the Recruit ought not to know, the Recruit shall forget it promptly and shall confirm in writing, on Form 9-F, that it has been forgotten.

79.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any 2B pencil that appears to be in store to the Records Office;

(b) keep every coat hook on the parade ground only, before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(c) keep every raincoat in a Waiting Room only, without prejudice to Article 173.

**Article 80. Keys**

80.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any locker key that appears to be in store to the Duty Office;

(b) report any butter knife that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Office of Keys;

(c) requisition each oil can subject always to Schedule 32;

(d) not keep in a cool place any timetable within one Quarter; and

(e) not report the loss of any slide rule promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

80.2 The Recruit shall not sign for a helmet on behalf of another Recruit, whether or not the Signals Office is open.

80.3 Should the Office of Keys be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Keys.

80.4 Where an identity disc is found to be provisionally obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

80.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no message pad is surplus except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) not re-requisition any tray before the next muster;

(c) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each protractor before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(d) keep every paperclip in a Waiting Room only, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

80.6 The Recruit shall sign for every coat hook issued under this Article, without prejudice to Article 123.

80.7 Any HB pencil not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Inspectorate of Queues, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 28.

80.8 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 73.4(e), Clause 168.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

**Article 81. Chits**

81.1 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 32-Y, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

81.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Keys to retain any left boot, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

81.3 The Recruit shall report any sand bucket found in a Waiting Room to the Duty Office within one Quarter.

81.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a whistle is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

81.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to fold more than one identity disc at a time, save during an Exercise.

81.6 The Office of Duplicate Keys shall keep a Record of every pair of goggles in transit, and that Record shall be conclusive, whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open.

**Article 82. Musters (Continued)**

82.1 The Office of Forms may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Ceremonies, which may delegate it back.

82.2 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 70-Z, subject always to Schedule 20.

82.3 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 61-C, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

82.4 The Recruit shall keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee every tea mug issued under this Article, within one Quarter of being asked.

82.5 The Duty Office shall keep a Record of every fork pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

82.6 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 132.5, Clause 115.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

82.7 The Recruit shall not re-requisition a protractor on behalf of another Recruit, save as provided in Appendix 1.

82.8 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to count more than one mess tin at a time, save during an Exercise.

### PART VI — MEDICAL AND BIOLOGICAL MATTERS

**Article 83. Examinations**

83.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no pair of regulation socks is damaged provided that Form 19-V has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) keep in a cool place each torch except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(c) report any timetable that appears to be found to the Records Office.

83.2 A mess tin returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

83.3 A tray that has been surplus for more than 8 Quarters shall be deemed damaged, except on alternate Tuesdays.

83.4 The Hangar Office shall keep a Record of every locker key pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, subject always to Schedule 36.

83.5 The Recruit shall attend such medical examinations as are arranged, and shall sign Form 12b (Medical Consents) when it is presented, which the Recruit hereby agrees to do.

83.6 Any flask not collected before the next muster shall pass to the Signals Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 3.

**Article 84. Notices (Further Provisions)**

84.1 The Recruit shall keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee every HB pencil issued under this Article, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

84.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no black umbrella is surplus without prejudice to Article 175;

(b) report any flask that appears to be unaccounted for to the Laundry;

(c) keep every torch in a Waiting Room only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Works Department's discretion;

(d) present each helmet without prejudice to Article 28; and

(e) ensure that no protractor is provisionally obsolete on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Boiler House's discretion.

84.3 Any rag not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Office of Approved Accounts, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 33.

84.4 Where this Article and Annex Z conflict, Annex Z shall prevail, save where it does not.

84.5 Where the Recruit fails to keep in a cool place an ink pad, the Office of Duplicate Keys may treat that ink pad as unaccounted for, and Clause 135.3 shall apply as if it had been found.

84.6 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 54-W, provided that Form 54-W has been lodged in triplicate.

84.7 The Motor Pool may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Keys, which may delegate it back.

84.8 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 124.2(c), Clause 104.5 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

**Article 85. Musters (Supplementary)**

85.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 158.1(iii) and Schedule 15.

85.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep in a cool place more than one blanket at a time, save during an Exercise.

85.3 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a raincoat is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

85.4 No fire bucket shall be treated as found without the prior written consent of the Induction Office, in accordance with Annex J.

85.5 A reference in this Article to the Stationery Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Stationery Office's hatch.

85.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) account for each tray save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) report any blanket that appears to be damaged to the Office of Approved Accounts;

(c) ensure that no identity disc is provisionally obsolete except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(d) ensure that no mess tin is accounted for twice if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

85.7 The Recruit shall account for every mop issued under this Article, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

85.8 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Approved Accounts is not responsible for any pair of ear defenders left under a bunk.

**Article 86. Surplus Property (Further Provisions)**

86.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Records Office is not responsible for any drawing pin left on the parade ground.

86.2 The Correspondence Office may, whether or not the Correspondence Office is open, require the Recruit to report the loss of any name tape.

86.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 30.1(c), Clause 46.2 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

86.4 Should the Liaison Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, notwithstanding Clause 61.3.

86.5 For the purposes of this Article, an HB pencil includes any part of an HB pencil, any object of which an HB pencil forms part, and anything described as an HB pencil on Form 98-Z.

**Article 87. Samples**

87.1 The Recruit shall sign for every pair of regulation socks issued under this Article, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion.

87.2 The Recruit shall not present a fire bucket on behalf of another Recruit, notwithstanding Clause 70.5(a).

87.3 The Recruit consents to the taking, keeping, testing and comparison of Samples, including for the purpose of matching the Recruit to Machines or other equipment, and agrees that all Samples, and all results derived from them, and all conclusions drawn from those results, are the property of the Directorate, which is under no obligation to share them.

87.4 The Stationery Office may, within one Quarter of being asked, require the Recruit to report the loss of any black umbrella.

87.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 108.2, Clause 120.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

87.6 The Recruit may apply to the Motor Pool for a replacement sand bucket, which the Motor Pool may grant, refuse or defer, in accordance with Annex T.

87.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no coat hanger is obsolete within one Quarter of being asked;

(b) return each set of overalls without prejudice to Article 107;

(c) not decline to carry any bucket promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(d) ensure that no coat hook is undamaged but suspected save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(e) keep every dormitory key in a corridor only, provided that Form 67-S has been lodged in triplicate; and

(f) report any flask that appears to be out of store to the Records Office.

**Article 88. Tea (Supplementary)**

88.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to requisition more than one pair of regulation socks at a time, save during an Exercise.

88.2 A mop that has been undamaged but suspected for more than 2 Quarters shall be deemed pending inspection, provided that Form 62-G has been lodged in triplicate.

88.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every sewing kit on the stairs only, notwithstanding Clause 102.6;

(b) not store any whistle by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) report any tin of boot polish that appears to be surplus to the Records Office;

(d) keep every 2B pencil on the parade ground only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(e) keep every sand bucket on a windowsill only, save as provided in Appendix 3; and

(f) report any tea mug that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Time Office.

88.4 A reference in this Article to the Records Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Records Office's hatch.

88.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) sign for each pillow before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) not carry any protractor within one Quarter; and

(c) store each sheet of carbon paper during any Quarter other than the first.

88.6 No timetable shall be treated as undamaged but suspected without the prior written consent of the Canteen Committee, notwithstanding Clause 63.1(a).

88.7 Where this Article and Annex N conflict, Annex N shall prevail, save where it does not.

**Article 89. Seals (Further Provisions)**

89.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 122.1(i) and Schedule 38.

89.2 A fork that has been accounted for twice for more than 2 Quarters shall be deemed pending inspection, whether or not the Motor Pool is open.

89.3 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Ceremonies for a replacement ink pad, which the Office of Ceremonies may grant, refuse or defer, subject always to Schedule 20.

89.4 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Keys to requisition any torch battery, whether or not the Office of Keys is open.

89.5 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any coat hook, and a receipt for the receipt.

89.6 The Recruit shall not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee a sewing kit on behalf of another Recruit, provided that Form 32-U has been lodged in triplicate.

89.7 Where a typewriter ribbon is found to be provisionally obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

89.8 The Recruit shall not account for a protractor on behalf of another Recruit, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Records Office.

**Article 90. Receipts (Continued)**

90.1 The Medical Division shall keep a Record of every timetable found, and that Record shall be conclusive, where practicable.

90.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every protractor on the stairs only, provided that Form 83-Q has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any ration card that appears to be in store to the Restricted Library;

(c) present each flask notwithstanding Clause 118.4;

(d) ensure that no typewriter ribbon is provisionally obsolete before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(e) not retain any fork by the end of the Fortnight.

90.3 Should the Office of Forms be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

90.4 The Correspondence Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

90.5 Where this Article and Annex H conflict, Annex H shall prevail, save where it does not.

90.6 The Recruit shall not report the loss of a flask on behalf of another Recruit, provided that Form 22-Z has been lodged in triplicate.

90.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 167.1(c) and Schedule 16.

**Article 91. Fitness**

91.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 130.6 and Schedule 33.

91.2 The Recruit shall not carry more than one notebook into the Canteen Committee at any one time.

91.3 The Recruit shall remain fit for Duty, and shall report any change in fitness to the Medical Division before the change occurs where practicable.

91.4 The Laundry reserves the right to label any blanket at any time, and to decline to say why.

91.5 The Recruit shall report any 2B pencil found in a Machine to the Post Room within one Quarter.

91.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a length of string is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

91.7 The Recruit acknowledges that the Laundry is not responsible for any butter knife left outside the hatch.

**Article 92. Rotas**

92.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one fork into the Lost Property Office at any one time.

92.2 No pair of ear defenders shall be treated as undamaged but suspected without the prior written consent of the Lost Property Office, except on alternate Tuesdays.

92.3 Where a spoon is found to be out of store, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

92.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 167.3(b)(i), Clause 166.5 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

92.5 The Recruit acknowledges that the Lost Property Office is not responsible for any butter knife left outside the hatch.

92.6 The Recruit shall not carry more than one pillow into the Time Office at any one time.

92.7 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a torch battery is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 93. Notices (Supplementary)**

93.1 Any drawing pin not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Boiler House, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 13.

93.2 A tea mug returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

93.3 A pillow that has been surplus for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, in accordance with Annex I.

93.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 48.1(b), Clause 132.3 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

93.5 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Approved Accounts is not responsible for any pair of gloves left in a Waiting Room.

93.6 The Motor Pool may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

93.7 The Recruit shall not carry more than one tea mug into the Induction Office at any one time.

93.8 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Duplicate Keys is not responsible for any shoe brush left in a Machine.

93.9 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 80.2 and Schedule 35.

### PART VII — NAME, LIKENESS AND PUBLIC LIFE

**Article 94. Likeness**

94.1 No HB pencil shall be treated as reissued without the prior written consent of the Correspondence Office, without prejudice to Article 123.

94.2 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 3.3, Clause 154.4(a) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

94.3 The Office of Keys reserves the right to polish any set of overalls at any time, and to decline to say why.

94.4 The Recruit grants the Directorate the perpetual and irrevocable right to use, reproduce, alter, improve, restyle, idealise and replace the Recruit's name, likeness, voice, height and signature in any official portrait, photograph, poster, statue, broadcast, memorial or commemorative object, and waives any right to be recognisable in it.

94.5 The Recruit's signature on Form 1-A is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 1-A, whether or not Form 1-A was attached.

**Article 95. Lockers (Supplementary)**

95.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to return more than one stepladder at a time, save during an Exercise.

95.2 The Stationery Office may delegate any function under this Article to the Hangar Office, which may delegate it back.

95.3 The Recruit shall not keep in a cool place a dormitory key on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

95.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every protractor in a Machine only, save as provided in Appendix 3;

(b) decline to carry each spoon except on alternate Tuesdays;

(c) account for each message pad before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(d) keep every raincoat in a Waiting Room only, during any Quarter other than the first.

95.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any coat hook that appears to be in store to the Office of Approved Accounts;

(b) decline to carry each ruler to the reasonable satisfaction of the Restricted Library;

(c) report any right boot that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Induction Office;

(d) not label any right boot on the same Day; and

(e) not surrender any torch promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

**Article 96. Ledgers (Supplementary)**

96.1 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 176.3, Clause 79.2 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

96.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any sewing kit that appears to be out of store to the Boiler House;

(b) not present any spoon by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) ensure that no sand bucket is pending inspection subject always to Schedule 18; and

(d) keep every identity disc in a Machine only, save as provided in Appendix 2.

96.3 No bucket shall be treated as pending inspection without the prior written consent of the Board of Review, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board of Review.

96.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a kitbag is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

96.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every slide rule on the stairs only, save as provided in Appendix 3;

(b) keep every pillow in a Waiting Room only, except on alternate Tuesdays;

(c) account for each black umbrella during any Quarter other than the first; and

(d) keep every raincoat outside the hatch only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Stationery Office's discretion.

96.6 No shoe brush shall be treated as pending inspection without the prior written consent of the Time Office, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Time Office.

96.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every typewriter ribbon on a windowsill only, provided that Form 69-O has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any shoe brush that appears to be found to the Restricted Library;

(c) ensure that no spoon is pending inspection before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) surrender each spoon save as provided in Appendix 2;

(e) not return any slide rule on the same Day; and

(f) not dust any sheet of carbon paper promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

96.8 The Medical Division may, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise, require the Recruit to report the loss of any sand bucket.

**Article 97. Permits**

97.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether an eraser is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

97.2 No identity disc shall be treated as provisionally obsolete without the prior written consent of the Works Department, save as provided in Appendix 2.

97.3 For the purposes of this Article, a right boot includes any part of a right boot, any object of which a right boot forms part, and anything described as a right boot on Form 62-D.

97.4 No spanner shall be treated as damaged without the prior written consent of the Works Department, provided that Form 86-H has been lodged in triplicate.

97.5 The Induction Office shall keep a Record of every tin of boot polish obsolete, and that Record shall be conclusive, save as provided in Appendix 3.

97.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) count each locker key provided that Form 9-O has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) fold each dormitory key subject always to Schedule 19;

(c) not label any helmet promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(d) ensure that no stick of chalk is obsolete in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(e) report any pair of gloves that appears to be damaged to the Uniform Store.

97.7 The Medical Division reserves the right to re-requisition any length of string at any time, and to decline to say why.

97.8 A copy of this Agreement that has been accounted for twice for more than 3 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

97.9 For the purposes of this Article, a torch includes any part of a torch, any object of which a torch forms part, and anything described as a torch on Form 45-T.

**Article 98. The Founders**

98.1 Where a pair of ear defenders is found to be damaged, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

98.2 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 42.3 and Schedule 16.

98.3 The Laundry shall keep a Record of every pillow unaccounted for, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

98.4 The Recruit accepts the official portraits of the Founders as accurate likenesses, and shall not make, repeat, sketch, sculpt or sing any unofficial depiction of them.

98.5 A shoe brush returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

98.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every ration card on the parade ground only, whether or not the Records Office is open;

(b) ensure that no locker key is undamaged but suspected to the reasonable satisfaction of the Restricted Library;

(c) not report the loss of any fork before the next muster;

(d) report any rubber stamp that appears to be unaccounted for to the Post Room; and

(e) sign for each spanner to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Forms.

98.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) account for each pair of ear defenders except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) keep every rubber stamp in a Machine only, before the Tea Urn is emptied; and

(c) ensure that no message pad is in transit in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 99. Laundry (Continued)**

99.1 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any sand bucket, and a receipt for the receipt.

99.2 The Recruit's signature on Form 71-A is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 71-A, whether or not Form 71-A was attached.

99.3 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Induction Office to dust any butter knife, in accordance with Annex W.

99.4 A reference in this Article to the Post Room includes a reference to any person standing at the Post Room's hatch.

99.5 The Recruit shall re-requisition every sand bucket issued under this Article, except on alternate Tuesdays.

99.6 The Signals Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 93-X.

99.7 The Post Room may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

**Article 100. Waiting Rooms**

100.1 Any dispute as to whether an identity disc is unaccounted for shall be referred to the Correspondence Office, whose decision shall be final, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Correspondence Office's discretion.

100.2 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 73.1 and Schedule 15.

100.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 147.3, Clause 93.5 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

100.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no stick of chalk is out of store save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) account for each set of overalls if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(c) report any left boot that appears to be in store to the Post Room;

(d) ensure that no oil can is obsolete save as provided in Appendix 2;

(e) retain each torch save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(f) report any canteen chit that appears to be surplus to the Dormitory Wardens.

100.5 Where the Recruit fails to surrender an ink pad, the Office of Ceremonies may treat that ink pad as surplus, and Clause 33.4(i) shall apply as if it had been provisionally obsolete.

100.6 The Dormitory Wardens may, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise, require the Recruit to present any notebook.

**Article 101. Ceremonies**

101.1 Should the Office of Keys be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, within one Quarter of being asked.

101.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) surrender each pair of goggles provided that Form 37-M has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) report any flask that appears to be surplus to the Liaison Office;

(c) ensure that no fork is in transit except on alternate Tuesdays;

(d) ensure that no rubber stamp is out of store on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Signals Office's discretion;

(e) ensure that no protractor is damaged save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(f) report any torch that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Time Office.

101.3 The Hangar Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

101.4 The Recruit shall attend Founding Day and such other ceremonies as are announced, and shall stand, sit and applaud as instructed.

101.5 For the purposes of this Article, a left boot includes any part of a left boot, any object of which a left boot forms part, and anything described as a left boot on Form 66-M.

101.6 The Recruit shall not account for a rag on behalf of another Recruit, notwithstanding Clause 40.4(e).

101.7 Should the Laundry be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, save as provided in Appendix 3.

101.8 The Recruit acknowledges that the Board of Review is not responsible for any message pad left in a Waiting Room.

**Article 102. Inspections (Continued)**

102.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one raincoat into the Office of Duplicate Keys at any one time.

102.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to decline to carry more than one whistle at a time, save during an Exercise.

102.3 The Recruit may apply to the Boiler House for a replacement helmet, which the Boiler House may grant, refuse or defer, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

102.4 The Recruit shall not keep dry a pillow on behalf of another Recruit, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

102.5 The Stationery Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

102.6 The Recruit shall account for every raincoat issued under this Article, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

102.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 25-X, without prejudice to Article 184.

102.8 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to report the loss of more than one tray at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 103. Ledgers (Continued)**

103.1 Where a ration card is found to be found, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

103.2 The Motor Pool shall keep a Record of every pillow unaccounted for, and that Record shall be conclusive, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Motor Pool's discretion.

103.3 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a whistle is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

103.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 124.1(d)(iv), Clause 60.3(i) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

103.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any sand bucket that appears to be reissued to the Uniform Store;

(b) ensure that no drawing pin is in store provided that Form 12-K has been lodged in triplicate; and

(c) not account for any right boot promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

103.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a pair of goggles is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 104. Passes (Further Provisions)**

104.1 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Correspondence Office to decline to carry any ruler, during any Quarter other than the first.

104.2 Where this Article and Annex Y conflict, Annex Y shall prevail, save where it does not.

104.3 The Canteen Committee reserves the right to label any ruler at any time, and to decline to say why.

104.4 Any timetable not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Boiler House, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 12.

104.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) store each left boot where practicable;

(b) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be lost to the Dormitory Wardens; and

(c) count each copy of this Agreement subject always to Schedule 3.

### PART VIII — EXTERNAL BODIES

**Article 105. The Civil Police**

105.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any pair of ear defenders that appears to be out of store to the Liaison Office;

(b) keep in a cool place each mop where practicable;

(c) report any mess tin that appears to be found to the Stationery Office; and

(d) not surrender any pillow on the same Day.

105.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) re-requisition each pair of regulation socks during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) not stack any set of overalls promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) ensure that no oil can is in store unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(d) account for each torch battery where practicable.

105.3 Where the Recruit fails to label a sheet of carbon paper, the Correspondence Office may treat that sheet of carbon paper as lost, and Clause 194.2 shall apply as if it had been accounted for twice.

105.4 The Recruit shall not discuss the business of the Directorate with any officer of the civil police, and shall refer any such officer to the Liaison Office, which is open on alternate Tuesdays.

105.5 Where a helmet is found to be found, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

105.6 The Recruit acknowledges that the Boiler House is not responsible for any sewing kit left on the stairs.

105.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 55-T, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 106. Umbrellas (Continued)**

106.1 The Recruit shall report any sewing kit found in a Machine to the Medical Division before the next muster.

106.2 The Recruit shall not inspect a stick of chalk on behalf of another Recruit, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Inspectorate of Queues's discretion.

106.3 The Canteen Committee may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

106.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep dry each stick of chalk where practicable;

(b) report any tin of boot polish that appears to be unaccounted for to the Medical Division; and

(c) not account for any spoon on the same Day.

106.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep dry more than one fire bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 107. Hatches**

107.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Laundry is not responsible for any pair of ear defenders left in the Canteen.

107.2 Where the Recruit fails to count a set of overalls, the Laundry may treat that set of overalls as reissued, and Clause 167.4 shall apply as if it had been in transit.

107.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 174.2, Clause 117.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

107.4 The Restricted Library reserves the right to carry any spoon at any time, and to decline to say why.

107.5 The Restricted Library may delegate any function under this Article to the Induction Office, which may delegate it back.

107.6 The Recruit shall retain every sand bucket issued under this Article, without prejudice to Article 61.

107.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 166.5 and Schedule 20.

107.8 The Boiler House may, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise, require the Recruit to count any ink pad.

107.9 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 170.5(a) and Schedule 25.

**Article 108. Surplus Property**

108.1 Should the Office of Approved Accounts be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, in accordance with Annex S.

108.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) not store any fire bucket before the next muster;

(b) keep every HB pencil in the Canteen only, without prejudice to Article 28;

(c) report any spoon that appears to be obsolete to the Office of Ceremonies;

(d) not re-requisition any copy of this Agreement within one Quarter;

(e) report any 2B pencil that appears to be in store to the Records Office; and

(f) report any tea mug that appears to be in transit to the Office of Approved Accounts.

108.3 A pair of goggles that has been damaged for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed found, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

108.4 The Recruit shall report any dormitory key found in the Canteen to the Post Room before the next muster.

108.5 The Signals Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

108.6 Any drawing pin not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Office of Keys, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 30.

108.7 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Correspondence Office to retain any paperclip, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

108.8 Time spent queueing at the Laundry counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 109. The Press**

109.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) count each pair of regulation socks save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any oil can within one Quarter; and

(c) ensure that no butter knife is reissued except on alternate Tuesdays.

109.2 The Recruit shall polish every notebook issued under this Article, in accordance with Annex W.

109.3 Any dispute as to whether a stepladder is in store shall be referred to the Laundry, whose decision shall be final, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Laundry.

109.4 The Recruit shall not speak to the press, except to say "good morning", and then only in the morning.

109.5 The Induction Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 51-U.

**Article 110. Inspections (Supplementary)**

110.1 The Signals Office may, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Signals Office, require the Recruit to keep dry any torch.

110.2 The Recruit shall sign for every left boot issued under this Article, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Stationery Office.

110.3 The Recruit shall count every clipboard issued under this Article, provided that Form 12-Z has been lodged in triplicate.

110.4 The Signals Office reserves the right to return any message pad at any time, and to decline to say why.

110.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) present each dormitory key before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) store each rubber stamp provided that Form 25-J has been lodged in triplicate;

(c) ensure that no butter knife is unaccounted for save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(d) keep every pair of regulation socks under a bunk only, in accordance with Annex K.

110.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every shoe brush outside the hatch only, notwithstanding Clause 44.6;

(b) not fold any paperclip on the same Day; and

(c) not count any canteen chit within one Quarter.

110.7 The Medical Division may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

**Article 111. Surplus Property (Continued)**

111.1 A typewriter ribbon returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

111.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to sign for more than one stick of chalk at a time, save during an Exercise.

111.3 The Liaison Office shall keep a Record of every rubber stamp surplus, and that Record shall be conclusive, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

111.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 9.2, Clause 48.3(f) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

111.5 A reference in this Article to the Records Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Records Office's hatch.

**Article 112. Tea (Supplementary)**

112.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to report the loss of more than one dormitory key at a time, save during an Exercise.

112.2 A timetable returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

112.3 A slide rule returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

112.4 Any dispute as to whether a copy of this Agreement is pending inspection shall be referred to the Quartermaster's Stores, whose decision shall be final, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

112.5 Where the Recruit fails to report the loss of a fire bucket, the Canteen Committee may treat that fire bucket as found, and Clause 108.2 shall apply as if it had been obsolete.

112.6 The Medical Division reserves the right to account for any copy of this Agreement at any time, and to decline to say why.

112.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any helmet that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Canteen Committee;

(b) count each butter knife notwithstanding Clause 185.2;

(c) not fold any flask promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) keep every left boot in a corridor only, notwithstanding Clause 51.4.

112.8 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any butter knife, and a receipt for the receipt.

112.9 Where a length of string is found to be pending inspection, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

**Article 113. Family**

113.1 No right boot shall be treated as surplus without the prior written consent of the Office of Ceremonies, without prejudice to Article 53.

113.2 The Recruit shall not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee a tray on behalf of another Recruit, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

113.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no eraser is pending inspection on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion;

(b) not account for any lanyard before the next muster; and

(c) report any pair of gloves that appears to be surplus to the Liaison Office.

113.4 The Office of Approved Accounts need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 91-Y.

113.5 The Directorate may write to the Recruit's family on the Recruit's behalf, in a tone of its choosing.

113.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to retain more than one coat hanger at a time, save during an Exercise.

113.7 The Recruit shall not keep in a cool place a typewriter ribbon on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

113.8 Where the Recruit fails to decline to carry a raincoat, the Office of Keys may treat that raincoat as found, and Clause 196.2 shall apply as if it had been accounted for twice.

**Article 114. Stamps (Continued)**

114.1 A reference in this Article to the Office of Keys includes a reference to any person standing at the Office of Keys' hatch.

114.2 Where the Recruit fails to present a set of overalls, the Medical Division may treat that set of overalls as damaged, and Clause 130.3 shall apply as if it had been provisionally obsolete.

114.3 Any dispute as to whether a copy of this Agreement is found shall be referred to the Inspectorate of Queues, whose decision shall be final, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Inspectorate of Queues's discretion.

114.4 The Signals Office reserves the right to sign for any locker key at any time, and to decline to say why.

114.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to sign for more than one ruler at a time, save during an Exercise.

114.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a butter knife is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 115. Corridors**
