115.1 For the purposes of this Article, a ruler includes any part of a ruler, any object of which a ruler forms part, and anything described as a ruler on Form 17-A.

115.2 Any dispute as to whether a name tape is in transit shall be referred to the Motor Pool, whose decision shall be final, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

115.3 The Motor Pool shall keep a Record of every mop lost, and that Record shall be conclusive, within one Quarter of being asked.

115.4 The Uniform Store shall keep a Record of every tray found, and that Record shall be conclusive, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

115.5 The Quartermaster's Stores may delegate any function under this Article to the Motor Pool, which may delegate it back.

115.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) retain each eraser to the reasonable satisfaction of the Records Office;

(b) ensure that no spoon is obsolete whether or not the Quartermaster's Stores is open;

(c) keep every right boot in a corridor only, save as provided in Appendix 1; and

(d) ensure that no oil can is in transit on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion.

115.7 The Quartermaster's Stores reserves the right to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any length of string at any time, and to decline to say why.

115.8 The Works Department may delegate any function under this Article to the Quartermaster's Stores, which may delegate it back.

### PART IX — ASSIGNMENT

**Article 116. Sections**

116.1 Where a right boot is found to be unaccounted for, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

116.2 A raincoat returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

116.3 The Recruit may apply to the Canteen Committee for a replacement oil can, which the Canteen Committee may grant, refuse or defer, without prejudice to Article 88.

116.4 The Recruit may be assigned to any Section, for any period, for any purpose, and consents in advance to assignment to a Section that is not listed in Schedule 2, has not yet been formed, or cannot be discussed.

116.5 Any drawing pin not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Works Department, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 20.

**Article 117. Trays**

117.1 Where a flask is found to be unaccounted for, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

117.2 A mop returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

117.3 Where this Article and Annex E conflict, Annex E shall prevail, save where it does not.

117.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any length of string that appears to be surplus to the Lost Property Office;

(b) not present any sheet of carbon paper by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) ensure that no rubber stamp is obsolete save as provided in Appendix 3; and

(d) not retain any right boot by the end of the Fortnight.

117.5 The Recruit shall not carry more than one bucket into the Laundry at any one time.

117.6 The Recruit shall not surrender a raincoat on behalf of another Recruit, in accordance with Annex P.

117.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 69-F, within one Quarter of being asked.

117.8 Where the Recruit fails to count a flask, the Quartermaster's Stores may treat that flask as in transit, and Clause 186.5 shall apply as if it had been surplus.

**Article 118. Laundry (Further Provisions)**

118.1 A coat hook returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

118.2 The Liaison Office may, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event, require the Recruit to polish any spoon.

118.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no ration card is reissued except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) keep every pillow in a Waiting Room only, except on alternate Tuesdays;

(c) report any pillow that appears to be in transit to the Office of Duplicate Keys;

(d) keep every sand bucket in the Canteen only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(e) not fold any timetable promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(f) ensure that no torch is obsolete before the Tea Urn is emptied.

118.4 The Works Department reserves the right to dust any length of string at any time, and to decline to say why.

118.5 Where this Article and Annex Y conflict, Annex Y shall prevail, save where it does not.

118.6 A name tape returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

118.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 64.5 and Schedule 30.

**Article 119. Corridors (Supplementary)**

119.1 The Office of Approved Accounts reserves the right to polish any canteen chit at any time, and to decline to say why.

119.2 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any flask, and a receipt for the receipt.

119.3 Where this Article and Annex C conflict, Annex C shall prevail, save where it does not.

119.4 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Keys for a replacement HB pencil, which the Office of Keys may grant, refuse or defer, notwithstanding Clause 37.3(c).

119.5 The Recruit shall not keep dry a mop on behalf of another Recruit, whether or not the Dormitory Wardens is open.

**Article 120. Favours**

120.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Quartermaster's Stores is not responsible for any torch battery left on the parade ground.

120.2 Any dispute as to whether a flask is accounted for twice shall be referred to the Post Room, whose decision shall be final, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Post Room.

120.3 The Inspectorate of Queues may, notwithstanding Clause 78.5, require the Recruit to count any helmet.

120.4 The Recruit agrees to perform one (1) favour for the Directorate, of the Directorate's choosing, at a time of the Directorate's choosing, without asking why, and acknowledges that the favour may be called in by any Office.

120.5 The Recruit shall report any pair of gloves found near the Tea Urn to the Time Office within one Quarter.

120.6 A 2B pencil that has been in transit for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed damaged, save as provided in Appendix 1.

120.7 Any dispute as to whether a pair of goggles is provisionally obsolete shall be referred to the Records Office, whose decision shall be final, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

**Article 121. Corridors (Supplementary)**

121.1 The Medical Division shall keep a Record of every pillow pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Medical Division's discretion.

121.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any torch that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Induction Office;

(b) not inspect any kitbag within one Quarter;

(c) keep every butter knife in the Canteen only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(d) report any 2B pencil that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Restricted Library;

(e) carry each pair of ear defenders where practicable; and

(f) label each blanket during any Quarter other than the first.

121.3 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a mop is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

121.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no tin of boot polish is surplus in accordance with Annex U;

(b) dust each tin of boot polish without prejudice to Article 121;

(c) ensure that no lanyard is undamaged but suspected notwithstanding Clause 107.4;

(d) report any butter knife that appears to be reissued to the Lost Property Office;

(e) not return any pair of goggles promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(f) ensure that no clipboard is out of store whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open.

121.5 The Restricted Library may, provided that Form 36-E has been lodged in triplicate, require the Recruit to store any sand bucket.

121.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to dust more than one left boot at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 122. Inspections**

122.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to requisition more than one whistle at a time, save during an Exercise.

122.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Uniform Store to count any length of string, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

122.3 A pair of gloves returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

122.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not dust any canteen chit on the same Day;

(b) not keep in a cool place any left boot by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) ensure that no message pad is lost in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(d) report any pair of ear defenders that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Lost Property Office; and

(e) keep every sewing kit in a Machine only, whether or not the Board of Review is open.

122.5 The Recruit's signature on Form 67-L is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 67-L, whether or not Form 67-L was attached.

122.6 A torch battery that has been found for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, during any Quarter other than the first.

122.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no protractor is pending inspection except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) ensure that no tray is damaged subject always to Schedule 30;

(c) ensure that no ration card is damaged within one Quarter of being asked;

(d) keep every ink pad in a Machine only, save as provided in Appendix 1; and

(e) dust each eraser where practicable.

**Article 123. Contact**

123.1 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 3.4(e), Clause 66.5(b) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

123.2 A rubber stamp that has been out of store for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed found, in accordance with Annex R.

123.3 The Recruit consents to being contacted at any hour by any person presenting a valid Form 7-V, and shall extend to that person every reasonable courtesy and the use of a chair.

123.4 The Recruit may apply to the Restricted Library for a replacement pair of goggles, which the Restricted Library may grant, refuse or defer, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Restricted Library.

123.5 Where the Recruit fails to report the loss of a torch, the Lost Property Office may treat that torch as in store, and Clause 162.4 shall apply as if it had been pending inspection.

123.6 Any 2B pencil not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Induction Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 40.

123.7 The Motor Pool shall keep a Record of every slide rule pending inspection, and that Record shall be conclusive, provided that Form 65-U has been lodged in triplicate.

**Article 124. Socks**

124.1 The Recruit shall not sign for a rag on behalf of another Recruit, except on alternate Tuesdays.

124.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Hangar Office to return any name tape, subject always to Schedule 35.

124.3 Should the Hangar Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, in accordance with Annex R.

124.4 The Recruit shall report any flask found on a windowsill to the Lost Property Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

124.5 The Works Department may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may delegate it back.

124.6 Where a whistle is found to be in transit, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

124.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) not fold any rag by the end of the Fortnight;

(b) keep every eraser in the Canteen only, notwithstanding Clause 180.2(d);

(c) not stack any stepladder before the next muster; and

(d) report any identity disc that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Post Room.

124.8 The Lost Property Office may, before the Tea Urn is emptied, require the Recruit to return any message pad.

**Article 125. Boots (Supplementary)**

125.1 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Dormitory Wardens to account for any fire bucket, notwithstanding Clause 34.2(d).

125.2 Any oil can not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Uniform Store, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 31.

125.3 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 61-W, without prejudice to Article 64.

125.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any protractor within one Quarter;

(b) keep in a cool place each pillow in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) ensure that no coat hanger is unaccounted for save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(d) report any length of string that appears to be in store to the Motor Pool.

125.5 No drawing pin shall be treated as obsolete without the prior written consent of the Quartermaster's Stores, during any Quarter other than the first.

**Article 126. Laundry (Supplementary)**

126.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 127.3(d) and Schedule 8.

126.2 Time spent queueing at the Correspondence Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

126.3 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Approved Accounts to re-requisition any timetable, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

126.4 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Approved Accounts to re-requisition any pair of regulation socks, except on alternate Tuesdays.

126.5 The Recruit may apply to the Correspondence Office for a replacement torch battery, which the Correspondence Office may grant, refuse or defer, whether or not the Correspondence Office is open.

126.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a drawing pin is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 127. Biscuit**

127.1 For the purposes of this Article, a raincoat includes any part of a raincoat, any object of which a raincoat forms part, and anything described as a raincoat on Form 48-F.

127.2 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 88-T, save as provided in Appendix 3.

127.3 Any Recruit who has read this far is entitled to one (1) biscuit, redeemable at the Canteen on presentation of Form 1-B(ii), which is not available.

127.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not decline to carry any tin of boot polish on the same Day;

(b) not surrender any rubber stamp on the same Day;

(c) report any raincoat that appears to be pending inspection to the Time Office;

(d) requisition each pair of ear defenders within one Quarter of being asked; and

(e) not polish any pair of regulation socks before the next muster.

127.5 No left boot shall be treated as unaccounted for without the prior written consent of the Records Office, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

127.6 The Signals Office reserves the right to label any torch at any time, and to decline to say why.

**Article 128. Surplus Property (Supplementary)**

128.1 The Office of Forms shall keep a Record of every canteen chit out of store, and that Record shall be conclusive, except on alternate Tuesdays.

128.2 Where this Article and Annex E conflict, Annex E shall prevail, save where it does not.

128.3 Where this Article and Annex A conflict, Annex A shall prevail, save where it does not.

128.4 The Recruit shall report any blanket found under a bunk to the Office of Forms by the end of the Fortnight.

128.5 Should the Liaison Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, provided that Form 12-O has been lodged in triplicate.

128.6 An ink pad returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

**Article 129. Passes (Supplementary)**

129.1 Any dispute as to whether a lanyard is obsolete shall be referred to the Inspectorate of Queues, whose decision shall be final, during any Quarter other than the first.

129.2 The Post Room may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Forms, which may delegate it back.

129.3 The Recruit shall re-requisition every fork issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 3.

129.4 The Recruit shall report any pair of ear defenders found in a Waiting Room to the Laundry on the same Day.

129.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 27.2, Clause 78.4(d) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

129.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every timetable in a Machine only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) inspect each mop in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(c) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any ruler by the end of the Fortnight;

(d) report any locker key that appears to be unaccounted for to the Restricted Library; and

(e) ensure that no clipboard is lost on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Inspectorate of Queues's discretion.

129.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any pair of ear defenders that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Office of Forms;

(b) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each tray whether or not the Uniform Store is open;

(c) surrender each flask except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(d) keep every butter knife outside the hatch only, notwithstanding Clause 78.3(b).

### PART X — ANIMALS AND STRAYS

**Article 130. Animals**

130.1 Any left boot not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Duty Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 4.

130.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to surrender more than one eraser at a time, save during an Exercise.

130.3 The Recruit shall not feed, name, shelter or keep any animal or stray found on or near Directorate property, and shall report any such animal or stray that appears to be following the Recruit.

130.4 A pillow that has been lost for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed in transit, where practicable.

130.5 The Laundry may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

130.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep dry more than one pair of goggles at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 131. Waiting Rooms (Continued)**

131.1 Where this Article and Annex V conflict, Annex V shall prevail, save where it does not.

131.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any shoe brush that appears to be reissued to the Office of Ceremonies;

(b) keep in a cool place each tin of boot polish within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) not keep dry any ration card promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) keep every pair of gloves outside the hatch only, within one Quarter of being asked.

131.3 Where this Article and Annex W conflict, Annex W shall prevail, save where it does not.

131.4 Time spent queueing at the Canteen Committee counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

131.5 The Recruit shall not report the loss of a timetable on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

131.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every pair of ear defenders outside the hatch only, without prejudice to Article 7;

(b) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be surplus to the Office of Approved Accounts;

(c) keep every sheet of carbon paper in a Machine only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Lost Property Office; and

(d) keep every stick of chalk in a Waiting Room only, in accordance with Annex N.

**Article 132. Clipboards (Continued)**

132.1 The Induction Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 61-C.

132.2 The Office of Ceremonies may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Approved Accounts, which may delegate it back.

132.3 The Recruit shall report any copy of this Agreement found on a windowsill to the Duty Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

132.4 The Works Department may, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit, require the Recruit to polish any sheet of carbon paper.

132.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no ink pad is provisionally obsolete in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(b) keep every ruler under a bunk only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise; and

(c) not keep in a cool place any coat hook promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

132.6 The Recruit shall label every set of overalls issued under this Article, in accordance with Annex A.

### PART XI — VARIATION, SURVIVAL AND REVIEW

**Article 133. Annex Q**

133.1 No slide rule shall be treated as damaged without the prior written consent of the Lost Property Office, within one Quarter of being asked.

133.2 The Inspectorate of Queues may, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit, require the Recruit to account for any pair of goggles.

133.3 Any raincoat not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Quartermaster's Stores, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 27.

133.4 The Recruit agrees to the terms of Annex Q, which will be notified to the Recruit when they become relevant, and acknowledges that they are agreed now.

133.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no sewing kit is reissued before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) present each spanner notwithstanding Clause 79.2(f); and

(c) report any name tape that appears to be found to the Duty Office.

133.6 The Induction Office shall keep a Record of every left boot out of store, and that Record shall be conclusive, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

**Article 134. Musters (Further Provisions)**

134.1 Should the Records Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, whether or not the Records Office is open.

134.2 The Recruit's signature on Form 50-V is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 50-V, whether or not Form 50-V was attached.

134.3 The Lost Property Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

134.4 The Dormitory Wardens reserves the right to report the loss of any butter knife at any time, and to decline to say why.

134.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to re-requisition more than one stepladder at a time, save during an Exercise.

134.6 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 159.1(e) and Schedule 1.

**Article 135. Permits (Supplementary)**

135.1 The Correspondence Office reserves the right to decline to carry any copy of this Agreement at any time, and to decline to say why.

135.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no torch is unaccounted for where practicable;

(b) report any flask that appears to be found to the Post Room;

(c) report the loss of each pair of ear defenders where practicable; and

(d) report any oil can that appears to be in transit to the Office of Forms.

135.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 182.6, Clause 54.4(c) and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

135.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 75.4(iii), Clause 146.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

135.5 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any dormitory key, and a receipt for the receipt.

135.6 The Recruit shall report any kitbag found in a Machine to the Quartermaster's Stores on the same Day.

135.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 100.6 and Schedule 35.

135.8 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 37-G, within one Quarter of being asked.

135.9 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any length of string, and a receipt for the receipt.

**Article 136. Variation**

136.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hook is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

136.2 For the purposes of this Article, a ration card includes any part of a ration card, any object of which a ration card forms part, and anything described as a ration card on Form 49-K.

136.3 The Records Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

136.4 In an emergency, as declared by the Directorate, any term of this Agreement may be varied by announcement, and an announcement made in any Canteen is an announcement made in every Canteen.

136.5 A torch battery returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

136.6 Should the Records Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 137. Carbon Paper (Supplementary)**

137.1 The Lost Property Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 76-H.

137.2 The Medical Division may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

137.3 Any rubber stamp not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Signals Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 19.

137.4 Where this Article and Annex U conflict, Annex U shall prevail, save where it does not.

137.5 Time spent queueing at the Duty Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

137.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) not present any flask on the same Day;

(b) keep every bucket in a Waiting Room only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Duplicate Keys; and

(c) not requisition any rubber stamp by the end of the Fortnight.

**Article 138. Precedence**

138.1 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to polish more than one dormitory key at a time, save during an Exercise.

138.2 The Recruit shall not sign for a right boot on behalf of another Recruit, during any Quarter other than the first.

138.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any clipboard that appears to be lost to the Quartermaster's Stores;

(b) carry each stick of chalk to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Ceremonies; and

(c) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be obsolete to the Laundry.

138.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to present more than one bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

138.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) dust each bucket where practicable;

(b) report any flask that appears to be surplus to the Correspondence Office;

(c) not stack any dormitory key by the end of the Fortnight;

(d) polish each pillow on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Approved Accounts's discretion; and

(e) fold each 2B pencil whether or not the Boiler House is open.

138.6 The Uniform Store shall keep a Record of every sand bucket unaccounted for, and that Record shall be conclusive, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 139. Duplicates**

139.1 No length of string shall be treated as out of store without the prior written consent of the Office of Forms, in accordance with Annex I.

139.2 The Board of Review reserves the right to decline to carry any ruler at any time, and to decline to say why.

139.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) report the loss of each set of overalls except on alternate Tuesdays;

(b) report any butter knife that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Records Office;

(c) not account for any HB pencil before the next muster;

(d) store each rag except on alternate Tuesdays;

(e) ensure that no torch battery is provisionally obsolete within one Quarter of being asked; and

(f) keep every left boot on the parade ground only, notwithstanding Clause 160.4.

139.4 The Recruit may apply to the Boiler House for a replacement canteen chit, which the Boiler House may grant, refuse or defer, provided that Form 67-G has been lodged in triplicate.

139.5 The Time Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

139.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every kitbag near the Tea Urn only, whether or not the Restricted Library is open;

(b) report any blanket that appears to be damaged to the Canteen Committee; and

(c) stack each typewriter ribbon unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

139.7 Should the Motor Pool be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, provided that Form 67-L has been lodged in triplicate.

139.8 Any lanyard not collected before the next muster shall pass to the Office of Duplicate Keys, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 1.

139.9 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a left boot is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

**Article 140. Hearings**

140.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) label each slide rule during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) keep every ration card outside the hatch only, save as provided in Appendix 3;

(c) ensure that no name tape is obsolete unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(d) report any blanket that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Laundry.

140.2 The Quartermaster's Stores reserves the right to return any stepladder at any time, and to decline to say why.

140.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 75.4(iv) and Schedule 2.

140.4 The Recruit shall attend any Hearing of the Board of Review to which the Recruit is summoned, and accepts the findings of any such Hearing in advance, on the understanding that this saves everybody time.

140.5 Any dispute as to whether a coat hanger is unaccounted for shall be referred to the Office of Duplicate Keys, whose decision shall be final, without prejudice to Article 100.

140.6 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any timetable, and a receipt for the receipt.

**Article 141. Socks (Further Provisions)**

141.1 Where this Article and Annex V conflict, Annex V shall prevail, save where it does not.

141.2 The Medical Division shall keep a Record of every rag reissued, and that Record shall be conclusive, save as provided in Appendix 2.

141.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every pair of goggles under a bunk only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) not count any locker key on the same Day;

(c) report any sewing kit that appears to be in transit to the Post Room;

(d) ensure that no pair of regulation socks is in transit within one Quarter of being asked; and

(e) ensure that no kitbag is unaccounted for subject always to Schedule 24.

141.4 Any dispute as to whether an identity disc is reissued shall be referred to the Office of Ceremonies, whose decision shall be final, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

141.5 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Liaison Office to dust any name tape, without prejudice to Article 28.

141.6 Any dormitory key not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Motor Pool, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 13.

**Article 142. Surplus Property**

142.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every ration card outside the hatch only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(b) ensure that no coat hanger is reissued provided that Form 40-Q has been lodged in triplicate;

(c) keep every locker key on a windowsill only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) fold each ink pad if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(e) keep every butter knife in a Waiting Room only, notwithstanding Clause 79.1; and

(f) present each spoon before the Tea Urn is emptied.

142.2 Should the Boiler House be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, save as provided in Appendix 2.

142.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 125.3, Clause 124.3 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

142.4 The Office of Ceremonies reserves the right to sign for any stick of chalk at any time, and to decline to say why.

142.5 No pillow shall be treated as unaccounted for without the prior written consent of the Office of Duplicate Keys, without prejudice to Article 13.

142.6 No lanyard shall be treated as surplus without the prior written consent of the Time Office, provided that Form 69-O has been lodged in triplicate.

142.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 23.5(d) and Schedule 33.

142.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every HB pencil on the stairs only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(b) keep every flask on the stairs only, where practicable;

(c) not inspect any message pad before the next muster;

(d) ensure that no sand bucket is unaccounted for except on alternate Tuesdays;

(e) ensure that no stick of chalk is pending inspection unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(f) ensure that no raincoat is undamaged but suspected provided that Form 55-T has been lodged in triplicate.

**Article 143. Lockers**

143.1 Where a tin of boot polish is found to be undamaged but suspected, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

143.2 Where the Recruit fails to carry a pair of goggles, the Records Office may treat that pair of goggles as in transit, and Clause 95.2 shall apply as if it had been lost.

143.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 122.5 and Schedule 32.

143.4 Time spent queueing at the Restricted Library counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

143.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) present each length of string before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) not present any butter knife promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) fold each pair of ear defenders before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) not store any length of string by the end of the Fortnight;

(e) keep dry each locker key where practicable; and

(f) re-requisition each set of overalls in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

143.6 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a locker key is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

143.7 The Recruit acknowledges that the Laundry is not responsible for any pair of regulation socks left in the Canteen.

143.8 Any dispute as to whether a tray is in transit shall be referred to the Office of Duplicate Keys, whose decision shall be final, notwithstanding Clause 47.4(b).

143.9 The Recruit shall report any coat hook found under a bunk to the Office of Ceremonies by the end of the Fortnight.

**Article 144. Survival**

144.1 The Recruit shall not carry more than one paperclip into the Motor Pool at any one time.

144.2 The Recruit shall not store a spanner on behalf of another Recruit, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

144.3 The terms of this Agreement survive the Recruit's discharge, transfer, promotion, retirement, and any later event.

144.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a pair of ear defenders is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

144.5 The Recruit acknowledges that the Uniform Store is not responsible for any whistle left under a bunk.

**Article 145. Stationery**

145.1 The Office of Keys reserves the right to keep in a cool place any dormitory key at any time, and to decline to say why.

145.2 Where this Article and Annex Z conflict, Annex Z shall prevail, save where it does not.

145.3 The Recruit shall not sign for a spoon on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

145.4 A reference in this Article to the Correspondence Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Correspondence Office's hatch.

145.5 Time spent queueing at the Time Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

145.6 A tin of boot polish that has been accounted for twice for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed in store, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

**Article 146. Seals (Continued)**

146.1 The Recruit acknowledges that the Uniform Store is not responsible for any name tape left in a corridor.

146.2 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a stepladder is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

146.3 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 67-G, during any Quarter other than the first.

146.4 A reference in this Article to the Board of Review includes a reference to any person standing at the Board of Review's hatch.

146.5 The Time Office shall keep a Record of every clipboard found, and that Record shall be conclusive, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Time Office's discretion.

146.6 A spanner that has been accounted for twice for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed surplus, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

146.7 The Recruit shall report any paperclip found near the Tea Urn to the Inspectorate of Queues by the end of the Fortnight.

146.8 The Office of Approved Accounts need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 56-A.

146.9 The Recruit's signature on Form 33-U is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 33-U, whether or not Form 33-U was attached.

**Article 147. Socks**

147.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a mess tin is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

147.2 No HB pencil shall be treated as found without the prior written consent of the Works Department, where practicable.

147.3 Should the Board of Review be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

147.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mess tin into the Stationery Office at any one time.

147.5 Where an oil can is found to be lost, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

### PART XII — GENERAL

**Article 148. Entire Agreement**

148.1 The Recruit shall report the loss of every coat hanger issued under this Article, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

148.2 The Office of Forms shall keep a Record of every sheet of carbon paper surplus, and that Record shall be conclusive, provided that Form 85-N has been lodged in triplicate.

148.3 The Recruit acknowledges that the Canteen Committee is not responsible for any oil can left outside the hatch.

148.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no coat hook is in transit save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) ensure that no eraser is found if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event; and

(c) keep dry each mop if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

148.5 This Agreement is the entire agreement between the parties, save for anything else the parties may be found to have agreed.

148.6 No rubber stamp shall be treated as pending inspection without the prior written consent of the Uniform Store, whether or not the Uniform Store is open.

148.7 Time spent queueing at the Office of Ceremonies counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

148.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) not fold any eraser before the next muster;

(b) keep every bucket in a corridor only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Lost Property Office; and

(c) not keep dry any set of overalls promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

**Article 149. Lockers (Further Provisions)**

149.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep dry any coat hanger promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) keep every butter knife in the Canteen only, without prejudice to Article 182;

(c) inspect each tin of boot polish before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) return each stepladder within one Quarter of being asked; and

(e) report any ruler that appears to be unaccounted for to the Office of Ceremonies.

149.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to store more than one sand bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

149.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no bucket is reissued subject always to Schedule 18;

(b) report any flask that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Lost Property Office;

(c) not sign for any stepladder promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(d) report any black umbrella that appears to be in store to the Motor Pool;

(e) ensure that no HB pencil is found where practicable; and

(f) present each notebook notwithstanding Clause 182.4(b).

149.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether an eraser is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

149.5 For the purposes of this Article, a coat hanger includes any part of a coat hanger, any object of which a coat hanger forms part, and anything described as a coat hanger on Form 51-U.

149.6 The Recruit shall report any identity disc found near the Tea Urn to the Works Department by the end of the Fortnight.

149.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every paperclip outside the hatch only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Board of Review;

(b) not requisition any drawing pin within one Quarter;

(c) not inspect any eraser before the next muster; and

(d) keep every copy of this Agreement in a corridor only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 150. Requisitions**

150.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no left boot is obsolete where practicable;

(b) not inspect any coat hanger by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) keep every pair of goggles on the stairs only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Correspondence Office's discretion;

(d) keep every HB pencil outside the hatch only, provided that Form 45-K has been lodged in triplicate;

(e) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any flask within one Quarter; and

(f) return each protractor if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

150.2 A reference in this Article to the Motor Pool includes a reference to any person standing at the Motor Pool's hatch.

150.3 Where a mop is found to be undamaged but suspected, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

150.4 A reference in this Article to the Time Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Time Office's hatch.

150.5 Where the Recruit fails to sign for a stepladder, the Office of Keys may treat that stepladder as pending inspection, and Clause 46.4(e) shall apply as if it had been obsolete.

150.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to store more than one kitbag at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 151. Time**

151.1 A whistle that has been lost for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed lost, without prejudice to Article 107.

151.2 The Recruit acknowledges that the Board of Review is not responsible for any left boot left in a Machine.

151.3 Where the Recruit fails to surrender a raincoat, the Time Office may treat that raincoat as damaged, and Clause 94.3 shall apply as if it had been unaccounted for.

151.4 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 74-S, within one Quarter of being asked.

151.5 The Directorate's clocks are correct. Time is of the essence, and the essence is the Directorate's.

151.6 The Recruit shall not count a spanner on behalf of another Recruit, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

**Article 152. Keys**

152.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) not inspect any left boot before the next muster;

(b) inspect each set of overalls where practicable; and

(c) keep every sand bucket on the stairs only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

152.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any set of overalls that appears to be found to the Medical Division;

(b) not decline to carry any spanner by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) keep every spanner on the stairs only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit;

(d) fold each eraser provided that Form 80-W has been lodged in triplicate; and

(e) report any black umbrella that appears to be found to the Quartermaster's Stores.

152.3 The Uniform Store may, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails, require the Recruit to keep dry any torch battery.

152.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one pair of gloves into the Boiler House at any one time.

152.5 The Recruit may apply to the Hangar Office for a replacement butter knife, which the Hangar Office may grant, refuse or defer, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 153. Vouchers**

153.1 Time spent queueing at the Lost Property Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

153.2 Any dispute as to whether a slide rule is damaged shall be referred to the Lost Property Office, whose decision shall be final, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

153.3 Where the Recruit fails to return a length of string, the Office of Keys may treat that length of string as provisionally obsolete, and Clause 110.1(iv) shall apply as if it had been out of store.

153.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any tea mug that appears to be surplus to the Office of Approved Accounts;

(b) keep every fork on a windowsill only, save as provided in Appendix 1;

(c) keep every spanner on the stairs only, subject always to Schedule 13;

(d) ensure that no pair of ear defenders is out of store notwithstanding Clause 87.6(d);

(e) report any sheet of carbon paper that appears to be unaccounted for to the Time Office; and

(f) ensure that no raincoat is provisionally obsolete notwithstanding Clause 30.1.

153.5 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 42.6, Clause 167.2 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

153.6 Time spent queueing at the Lost Property Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

153.7 The Recruit shall not decline to carry an identity disc on behalf of another Recruit, during any Quarter other than the first.

153.8 Any dispute as to whether a flask is out of store shall be referred to the Canteen Committee, whose decision shall be final, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

153.9 The Correspondence Office shall keep a Record of every paperclip found, and that Record shall be conclusive, save as provided in Appendix 2.

**Article 154. Laundry**

154.1 Where the Recruit fails to sign for a slide rule, the Office of Forms may treat that slide rule as reissued, and Clause 113.6(d)(iv) shall apply as if it had been lost.

154.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no flask is lost to the reasonable satisfaction of the Records Office;

(b) keep every raincoat in the Canteen only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) keep every helmet in a Machine only, without prejudice to Article 110;

(d) ensure that no slide rule is out of store subject always to Schedule 4;

(e) report any paperclip that appears to be accounted for twice to the Boiler House; and

(f) ensure that no flask is out of store on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Signals Office's discretion.

154.3 The Uniform Store reserves the right to count any protractor at any time, and to decline to say why.

154.4 Where a mess tin is found to be surplus, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

154.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every ruler in the Canteen only, in accordance with Annex D;

(b) ensure that no coat hook is unaccounted for in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(c) keep every helmet near the Tea Urn only, where practicable.

154.6 No 2B pencil shall be treated as pending inspection without the prior written consent of the Motor Pool, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Motor Pool's discretion.

154.7 The Lost Property Office reserves the right to label any rubber stamp at any time, and to decline to say why.

**Article 155. Headings**

155.1 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 98.4, Clause 112.3 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

155.2 The Recruit acknowledges that the Signals Office is not responsible for any name tape left under a bunk.

155.3 Headings are for convenience only, and the Recruit shall not rely on them, or on anything else in this Agreement for convenience.

155.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one sand bucket into the Stationery Office at any one time.

155.5 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any sand bucket, and a receipt for the receipt.

155.6 The Canteen Committee need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 62-D.

155.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) not surrender any pillow before the next muster;

(b) return each raincoat in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(c) ensure that no pair of goggles is reissued if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

**Article 156. Stationery**

156.1 Any whistle not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Uniform Store, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 15.

156.2 The Recruit's signature on Form 68-U is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 68-U, whether or not Form 68-U was attached.

156.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) not re-requisition any eraser promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) report any blanket that appears to be lost to the Stationery Office;

(c) present each tea mug except on alternate Tuesdays;

(d) keep every pair of gloves on the parade ground only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(e) not sign for any spanner before the next muster; and

(f) ensure that no tin of boot polish is accounted for twice unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

156.4 Where the Recruit fails to keep in a cool place an eraser, the Post Room may treat that eraser as reissued, and Clause 59.1(d) shall apply as if it had been surplus.

156.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each timetable if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(b) not account for any whistle before the next muster;

(c) not return any mop before the next muster;

(d) report any butter knife that appears to be pending inspection to the Duty Office;

(e) not keep dry any clipboard on the same Day; and

(f) ensure that no HB pencil is accounted for twice in accordance with Annex M.

156.6 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Board of Review to fold any blanket, where practicable.

156.7 No 2B pencil shall be treated as in store without the prior written consent of the Quartermaster's Stores, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

**Article 157. Queues (Supplementary) (Further Provisions)**

157.1 Any ration card not collected within one Quarter shall pass to the Dormitory Wardens, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 16.

157.2 Where a torch battery is found to be accounted for twice, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

157.3 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to decline to carry more than one fire bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

157.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a slide rule is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

157.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every notebook in a corridor only, without prejudice to Article 173;

(b) not sign for any sewing kit on the same Day;

(c) ensure that no coat hook is reissued before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) stack each sheet of carbon paper except on alternate Tuesdays; and

(e) not return any locker key promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

157.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any sewing kit that appears to be reissued to the Office of Ceremonies;

(b) not carry any rag promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(c) report any shoe brush that appears to be unaccounted for to the Office of Ceremonies;

(d) report any slide rule that appears to be pending inspection to the Records Office; and

(e) carry each flask where practicable.

157.7 Any rag not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Medical Division, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 31.

157.8 The Recruit shall report any coat hanger found in a corridor to the Hangar Office within one Quarter.

157.9 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 85.2 and Schedule 30.

**Article 158. Boots (Continued)**

158.1 Any torch battery not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Motor Pool, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 12.

158.2 Any dispute as to whether a timetable is reissued shall be referred to the Quartermaster's Stores, whose decision shall be final, in accordance with Annex V.

158.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no fork is undamaged but suspected notwithstanding Clause 26.1;

(b) not dust any butter knife within one Quarter;

(c) ensure that no pair of ear defenders is out of store if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(d) report any clipboard that appears to be reissued to the Restricted Library; and

(e) ensure that no paperclip is lost within one Quarter of being asked.

158.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one slide rule into the Post Room at any one time.

158.5 A pair of ear defenders returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

158.6 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 186.3(a) and Schedule 33.

158.7 The Records Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 73-B.

158.8 Where the Recruit fails to keep in a cool place a drawing pin, the Office of Approved Accounts may treat that drawing pin as in transit, and Clause 18.4 shall apply as if it had been surplus.

**Article 159. Signature**

159.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a pair of goggles is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

159.2 Any dispute as to whether a lanyard is unaccounted for shall be referred to the Medical Division, whose decision shall be final, within one Quarter of being asked.

159.3 The Recruit acknowledges that the Office of Ceremonies is not responsible for any left boot left outside the hatch.

159.4 The Recruit signs this Agreement by pressing the button marked I AGREE, and acknowledges that the button is a signature, a seal, and a witness.

159.5 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mop into the Works Department at any one time.

159.6 The Board of Review reserves the right to dust any flask at any time, and to decline to say why.

159.7 The Recruit shall report any fork found on the stairs to the Medical Division promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

**Article 160. Corridors (Continued)**

160.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no identity disc is pending inspection save as provided in Appendix 3;

(b) report any timetable that appears to be damaged to the Boiler House; and

(c) ensure that no bucket is obsolete in accordance with Annex M.

160.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Duplicate Keys to account for any pair of goggles, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Duplicate Keys.

160.3 The Boiler House may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

160.4 Any dispute as to whether a slide rule is in transit shall be referred to the Duty Office, whose decision shall be final, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

160.5 For the purposes of this Article, an identity disc includes any part of an identity disc, any object of which an identity disc forms part, and anything described as an identity disc on Form 2-N.

**Article 161. Receipts (Further Provisions)**

161.1 The Time Office may, notwithstanding Clause 172.6(iii), require the Recruit to inspect any pair of goggles.

161.2 The Dormitory Wardens may, notwithstanding Clause 73.3(a)(iv), require the Recruit to carry any stick of chalk.

161.3 Should the Correspondence Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, where practicable.

161.4 The Recruit may apply to the Office of Duplicate Keys for a replacement spanner, which the Office of Duplicate Keys may grant, refuse or defer, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

161.5 A spanner that has been provisionally obsolete for more than 5 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Ceremonies's discretion.

161.6 The Recruit acknowledges that the Liaison Office is not responsible for any pillow left in the Canteen.

161.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no sheet of carbon paper is pending inspection in accordance with Annex D;

(b) keep every left boot on the stairs only, save as provided in Appendix 3;

(c) ensure that no timetable is reissued on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Dormitory Wardens's discretion; and

(d) report any slide rule that appears to be in store to the Hangar Office.

161.8 The Induction Office may, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Induction Office, require the Recruit to account for any set of overalls.

161.9 The Recruit acknowledges that the Canteen Committee is not responsible for any coat hook left on the parade ground.

**Article 162. Rotas (Further Provisions)**

162.1 A shoe brush that has been provisionally obsolete for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed lost, where practicable.

162.2 Where the Recruit fails to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee a sheet of carbon paper, the Records Office may treat that sheet of carbon paper as undamaged but suspected, and Clause 65.5(d) shall apply as if it had been undamaged but suspected.

162.3 A message pad returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

162.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not retain any coat hook before the next muster;

(b) ensure that no whistle is surplus to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Duplicate Keys; and

(c) present each torch battery save as provided in Appendix 1.

162.5 For the purposes of this Article, a timetable includes any part of a timetable, any object of which a timetable forms part, and anything described as a timetable on Form 39-N.

162.6 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any ruler, and a receipt for the receipt.

162.7 A helmet returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

162.8 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 39.4(a) and Schedule 22.

### PART XIII — SUPPLEMENTARY PROVISIONS

**Article 163. Passes (Supplementary)**

163.1 The Office of Keys may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

163.2 The Recruit shall report any typewriter ribbon found outside the hatch to the Canteen Committee on the same Day.

163.3 A fork returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

163.4 Where an HB pencil is found to be obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

163.5 The Recruit may apply to the Boiler House for a replacement HB pencil, which the Boiler House may grant, refuse or defer, during any Quarter other than the first.

163.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every name tape in the Canteen only, notwithstanding Clause 151.2(iii);

(b) keep every typewriter ribbon under a bunk only, whether or not the Canteen Committee is open;

(c) ensure that no timetable is damaged on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Records Office's discretion;

(d) polish each rubber stamp on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Induction Office's discretion; and

(e) not stack any coat hook within one Quarter.

163.7 A mess tin returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

**Article 164. Stationery (Supplementary)**

164.1 A ration card that has been out of store for more than 6 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, subject always to Schedule 6.

164.2 Where the Recruit fails to account for a whistle, the Time Office may treat that whistle as accounted for twice, and Clause 17.5(iii) shall apply as if it had been undamaged but suspected.

164.3 The Induction Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

164.4 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 54-M, during any Quarter other than the first.

164.5 Time spent queueing at the Boiler House counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

164.6 A tray returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

164.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no mess tin is found where practicable;

(b) polish each bucket subject always to Schedule 27;

(c) not return any 2B pencil promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) ensure that no 2B pencil is obsolete provided that Form 51-C has been lodged in triplicate.

**Article 165. Vouchers**

165.1 A reference in this Article to the Works Department includes a reference to any person standing at the Works Department's hatch.

165.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep dry more than one sand bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

165.3 No drawing pin shall be treated as accounted for twice without the prior written consent of the Dormitory Wardens, without prejudice to Article 138.

165.4 The Recruit shall not surrender a spanner on behalf of another Recruit, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise.

165.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every raincoat under a bunk only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(b) ensure that no sewing kit is surplus notwithstanding Clause 81.1;

(c) report any sewing kit that appears to be accounted for twice to the Lost Property Office;

(d) surrender each pair of ear defenders provided that Form 55-T has been lodged in triplicate; and

(e) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each timetable during any Quarter other than the first.

165.6 The Recruit shall inspect every stick of chalk issued under this Article, during any Quarter other than the first.

165.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 72.1 and Schedule 16.

165.8 The Recruit shall report any rag found in the Canteen to the Duty Office within one Quarter.

165.9 Any tray not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Records Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 35.

165.10 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any locker key that appears to be reissued to the Lost Property Office;

(b) not carry any locker key on the same Day;

(c) report any identity disc that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Boiler House;

(d) not carry any stepladder within one Quarter; and

(e) ensure that no ink pad is lost in accordance with Annex B.

**Article 166. Rotas (Supplementary)**

166.1 The Recruit may apply to the Dormitory Wardens for a replacement pair of ear defenders, which the Dormitory Wardens may grant, refuse or defer, where practicable.

166.2 The Correspondence Office may delegate any function under this Article to the Post Room, which may delegate it back.

166.3 The Recruit's signature on Form 51-O is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 51-O, whether or not Form 51-O was attached.

166.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every paperclip in the Canteen only, provided that Form 9-F has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) keep every oil can outside the hatch only, notwithstanding Clause 54.4(b);

(c) ensure that no name tape is surplus where practicable;

(d) keep dry each black umbrella to the reasonable satisfaction of the Records Office; and

(e) not store any blanket within one Quarter.

166.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) not label any dormitory key within one Quarter;

(b) re-requisition each protractor notwithstanding Clause 117.3(f)(i); and

(c) keep every ruler outside the hatch only, notwithstanding Clause 54.5.

166.6 The Recruit acknowledges that the Inspectorate of Queues is not responsible for any copy of this Agreement left near the Tea Urn.

166.7 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any torch battery on the same Day;

(b) keep every tin of boot polish in a Machine only, within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) not requisition any tray promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(d) report any pair of regulation socks that appears to be surplus to the Post Room;

(e) keep every length of string on the parade ground only, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event; and

(f) ensure that no rag is in transit before the Tea Urn is emptied.

166.8 The Recruit shall report any spoon found on the parade ground to the Lost Property Office on the same Day.

166.9 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 88.6, Clause 156.3(b) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

**Article 167. Requisitions**

167.1 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hook is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

167.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Duplicate Keys to inspect any ruler, if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event.

167.3 The Laundry reserves the right to keep in a cool place any pair of gloves at any time, and to decline to say why.

167.4 For the purposes of this Article, an ink pad includes any part of an ink pad, any object of which an ink pad forms part, and anything described as an ink pad on Form 4-U.

167.5 Where the Recruit fails to count a typewriter ribbon, the Works Department may treat that typewriter ribbon as surplus, and Clause 99.4 shall apply as if it had been out of store.

167.6 Should the Inspectorate of Queues be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, subject always to Schedule 35.

167.7 A timetable that has been damaged for more than 6 Quarters shall be deemed obsolete, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

167.8 Any black umbrella not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Quartermaster's Stores, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 8.

**Article 168. Trays (Supplementary)**

168.1 Time spent queueing at the Hangar Office counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

168.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Signals Office to surrender any stick of chalk, notwithstanding Clause 109.4.

168.3 A rag returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

168.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to decline to carry more than one bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

168.5 The Signals Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 65-P.

168.6 The Induction Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 66-M.

168.7 Time spent queueing at the Quartermaster's Stores counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 169. Socks (Continued)**

169.1 Time spent queueing at the Office of Ceremonies counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

169.2 The Canteen Committee need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 93-X.

169.3 Where the Recruit fails to sign for a helmet, the Hangar Office may treat that helmet as out of store, and Clause 22.2 shall apply as if it had been out of store.

169.4 For the purposes of this Article, a slide rule includes any part of a slide rule, any object of which a slide rule forms part, and anything described as a slide rule on Form 32-X.

169.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any sewing kit that appears to be pending inspection to the Office of Forms;

(b) ensure that no pair of gloves is unaccounted for if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(c) fold each fire bucket if so requested, and if not so requested, then in any event;

(d) keep in a cool place each eraser in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(e) report any sand bucket that appears to be out of store to the Restricted Library.

169.6 The Recruit shall count every torch issued under this Article, save as provided in Appendix 2.

169.7 A rag that has been unaccounted for for more than 9 Quarters shall be deemed unaccounted for, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Laundry.

169.8 The Works Department reserves the right to label any pair of goggles at any time, and to decline to say why.

### PART XIV — STANDING ORDERS INCORPORATED BY REFERENCE

**Article 170. Corridors**

170.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every pair of ear defenders on a windowsill only, on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Office of Ceremonies's discretion;

(b) keep every black umbrella under a bunk only, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Office of Duplicate Keys; and

(c) report any stick of chalk that appears to be in store to the Uniform Store.

170.2 Time spent queueing at the Inspectorate of Queues counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

170.3 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any kitbag, and a receipt for the receipt.

170.4 Time spent queueing at the Office of Approved Accounts counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

170.5 For the purposes of this Article, a slide rule includes any part of a slide rule, any object of which a slide rule forms part, and anything described as a slide rule on Form 40-U.

170.6 The Works Department need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 37-G.

170.7 Should the Office of Forms be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

170.8 The Recruit's signature on Form 82-N is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 82-N, whether or not Form 82-N was attached.

170.9 The Recruit shall not carry more than one typewriter ribbon into the Office of Approved Accounts at any one time.

**Article 171. Returns**

171.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) not count any pair of goggles promptly, and in any case before Thursday;

(b) report any timetable that appears to be pending inspection to the Correspondence Office; and

(c) report any bucket that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Office of Ceremonies.

171.2 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 55.2 and Schedule 15.

171.3 The Recruit's signature on Form 47-Y is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 47-Y, whether or not Form 47-Y was attached.

171.4 The Recruit's signature on Form 44-U is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 44-U, whether or not Form 44-U was attached.

171.5 The Motor Pool reserves the right to retain any slide rule at any time, and to decline to say why.

171.6 The Canteen Committee may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

171.7 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any sand bucket, and a receipt for the receipt.

**Article 172. Queues (Supplementary)**

172.1 Where this Article and Annex Y conflict, Annex Y shall prevail, save where it does not.

172.2 The Recruit shall report any stepladder found on a windowsill to the Records Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

172.3 A reference in this Article to the Boiler House includes a reference to any person standing at the Boiler House's hatch.

172.4 The Recruit shall not carry more than one ration card into the Works Department at any one time.

172.5 Where this Article and Annex F conflict, Annex F shall prevail, save where it does not.

172.6 Any notebook not collected on the same Day shall pass to the Liaison Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 7.

172.7 Should the Office of Approved Accounts be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, within one Quarter of being asked.

172.8 The Office of Approved Accounts reserves the right to count any right boot at any time, and to decline to say why.

172.9 Time spent queueing at the Medical Division counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

**Article 173. Hatches (Continued)**

173.1 The Works Department may delegate any function under this Article to the Motor Pool, which may delegate it back.

173.2 No stepladder shall be treated as surplus without the prior written consent of the Records Office, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

173.3 No fork shall be treated as reissued without the prior written consent of the Lost Property Office, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Lost Property Office.

173.4 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hanger is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

173.5 The Works Department reserves the right to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any left boot at any time, and to decline to say why.

173.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any name tape that appears to be reissued to the Uniform Store;

(b) keep every tray on a windowsill only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(c) keep every sewing kit near the Tea Urn only, provided that Form 80-W has been lodged in triplicate; and

(d) ensure that no pair of ear defenders is in store save as provided in Appendix 3.

**Article 174. Clipboards**

174.1 The Medical Division may delegate any function under this Article to the Inspectorate of Queues, which may delegate it back.

174.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to dust more than one left boot at a time, save during an Exercise.

174.3 The Recruit's signature on Form 32-X is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 32-X, whether or not Form 32-X was attached.

174.4 The Recruit shall report any sand bucket found on the stairs to the Records Office within one Quarter.

174.5 A reference in this Article to the Board of Review includes a reference to any person standing at the Board of Review's hatch.

174.6 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 23.6(d), Clause 148.1 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

174.7 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 45-T, whether or not the Motor Pool is open.

174.8 The Inspectorate of Queues may delegate any function under this Article to the Office of Ceremonies, which may delegate it back.

174.9 The Recruit acknowledges that the Records Office is not responsible for any shoe brush left in a corridor.

**Article 175. Queues (Supplementary) (Supplementary)**

175.1 Time spent queueing at the Office of Keys counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

175.2 A reference in this Article to the Dormitory Wardens includes a reference to any person standing at the Dormitory Wardens' hatch.

175.3 A reference in this Article to the Office of Ceremonies includes a reference to any person standing at the Office of Ceremonies' hatch.

175.4 The Induction Office may, where practicable, require the Recruit to carry any tin of boot polish.

175.5 The Recruit acknowledges that the Records Office is not responsible for any kitbag left in the Canteen.

175.6 Where a stepladder is found to be unaccounted for, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

175.7 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Records Office to fold any coat hanger, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

175.8 The Recruit shall not report the loss of a set of overalls on behalf of another Recruit, during any Quarter other than the first.

175.9 The Hangar Office shall keep a Record of every mess tin damaged, and that Record shall be conclusive, except on alternate Tuesdays.

**Article 176. Musters (Continued)**

176.1 The Office of Duplicate Keys may, whether or not the Office of Duplicate Keys is open, require the Recruit to report the loss of any copy of this Agreement.

176.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep in a cool place more than one spanner at a time, save during an Exercise.

176.3 The Recruit shall report any slide rule found in a Waiting Room to the Inspectorate of Queues on the same Day.

176.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every fork in a Machine only, in accordance with Annex D;

(b) not decline to carry any spanner on the same Day; and

(c) not return any dormitory key promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

176.5 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 50-V, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Time Office.

176.6 The Medical Division may delegate any function under this Article to the Canteen Committee, which may delegate it back.

176.7 The Recruit may apply to the Signals Office for a replacement tin of boot polish, which the Signals Office may grant, refuse or defer, within one Quarter of being asked.

176.8 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Canteen Committee to polish any drawing pin, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

176.9 The Recruit acknowledges that the Laundry is not responsible for any kitbag left on the parade ground.

**Article 177. Carbon Paper (Continued)**

177.1 The Recruit's signature on Form 94-X is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 94-X, whether or not Form 94-X was attached.

177.2 Where the Recruit fails to keep in a cool place a clipboard, the Medical Division may treat that clipboard as reissued, and Clause 109.2(d) shall apply as if it had been undamaged but suspected.

177.3 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a coat hanger is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

177.4 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to surrender more than one butter knife at a time, save during an Exercise.

177.5 The Recruit's signature on Form 76-H is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 76-H, whether or not Form 76-H was attached.

177.6 The Uniform Store may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

177.7 A helmet that has been out of store for more than 7 Quarters shall be deemed in transit, subject always to Schedule 15.

177.8 The Recruit shall not carry more than one black umbrella into the Inspectorate of Queues at any one time.

**Article 178. Keys (Further Provisions)**

178.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every oil can in a Machine only, save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(b) ensure that no mop is in store on the next working Day, or the Day after, at the Post Room's discretion; and

(c) not return any HB pencil within one Quarter.

178.2 A reference in this Article to the Office of Forms includes a reference to any person standing at the Office of Forms' hatch.

178.3 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Hangar Office to keep dry any shoe brush, during any Quarter other than the first.

178.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 151.6, Clause 88.3 and, where relevant, Appendix 2.

178.5 The Recruit shall report any lanyard found on the parade ground to the Quartermaster's Stores within one Quarter.

178.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to stack more than one pair of regulation socks at a time, save during an Exercise.

178.7 Where the Recruit fails to dust a rubber stamp, the Records Office may treat that rubber stamp as in transit, and Clause 151.1 shall apply as if it had been in store.

178.8 The Recruit acknowledges that the Liaison Office is not responsible for any fork left near the Tea Urn.

178.9 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any pair of gloves that appears to be reissued to the Dormitory Wardens;

(b) retain each right boot save where the Duty Officer directs otherwise;

(c) not report the loss of any ration card promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) ensure that no pair of gloves is unaccounted for in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

178.10 Any dispute as to whether a left boot is damaged shall be referred to the Office of Ceremonies, whose decision shall be final, subject always to Schedule 31.

### PART XV — TRANSITIONAL PROVISIONS

**Article 179. Seals (Further Provisions)**
