179.1 Any rag not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Motor Pool, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 26.

179.2 The Motor Pool may delegate any function under this Article to the Induction Office, which may delegate it back.

179.3 Any fork not collected by the end of the Fortnight shall pass to the Time Office, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 19.

179.4 Any dispute as to whether a pair of gloves is accounted for twice shall be referred to the Boiler House, whose decision shall be final, subject always to Schedule 28.

179.5 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 30-B, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

179.6 The Motor Pool may delegate any function under this Article to the Time Office, which may delegate it back.

179.7 The Recruit shall report any shoe brush found near the Tea Urn to the Laundry promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

179.8 Where the Recruit fails to dust a flask, the Hangar Office may treat that flask as in store, and Clause 147.6 shall apply as if it had been accounted for twice.

179.9 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 67.5, Clause 2.4(f) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

179.10 The Recruit shall report any pair of goggles found in the Canteen to the Quartermaster's Stores by the end of the Fortnight.

**Article 180. Lost Property (Continued)**

180.1 A reference in this Article to the Boiler House includes a reference to any person standing at the Boiler House's hatch.

180.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to re-requisition more than one message pad at a time, save during an Exercise.

180.3 The Recruit acknowledges that the Dormitory Wardens is not responsible for any rag left in the Canteen.

180.4 Time spent queueing at the Medical Division counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

180.5 The Recruit shall not carry more than one slide rule into the Office of Ceremonies at any one time.

180.6 Where the Recruit fails to decline to carry a stick of chalk, the Liaison Office may treat that stick of chalk as in transit, and Clause 53.3 shall apply as if it had been surplus.

180.7 The Restricted Library may, subject always to Schedule 39, require the Recruit to decline to carry any sand bucket.

180.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every rubber stamp in a corridor only, notwithstanding Clause 65.1(b);

(b) keep every blanket under a bunk only, save as provided in Appendix 1;

(c) keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee each copy of this Agreement save as provided in Appendix 3;

(d) report any torch battery that appears to be undamaged but suspected to the Records Office;

(e) ensure that no sewing kit is undamaged but suspected provided that Form 50-P has been lodged in triplicate; and

(f) keep every sand bucket on the stairs only, except on alternate Tuesdays.

180.9 Where a bucket is found to be accounted for twice, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

180.10 The Restricted Library need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 88-T.

**Article 181. Socks (Continued)**

181.1 The Induction Office reserves the right to polish any notebook at any time, and to decline to say why.

181.2 A 2B pencil returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

181.3 The Hangar Office may, notwithstanding Clause 54.6, require the Recruit to label any dormitory key.

181.4 Where the Recruit fails to re-requisition a shoe brush, the Correspondence Office may treat that shoe brush as pending inspection, and Clause 45.6(c) shall apply as if it had been obsolete.

181.5 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 33.4(e) and Schedule 10.

181.6 Time spent queueing at the Post Room counts as time on Duty, save where the queue is unauthorised.

181.7 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 103.2 and Schedule 37.

181.8 The Recruit shall not carry more than one sand bucket into the Liaison Office at any one time.

181.9 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 94-X, except on alternate Tuesdays.

181.10 A coat hook that has been reissued for more than 3 Quarters shall be deemed undamaged but suspected, within one Quarter of being asked.

**Article 182. Laundry (Supplementary)**

182.1 A reference in this Article to the Medical Division includes a reference to any person standing at the Medical Division's hatch.

182.2 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Induction Office to decline to carry any ruler, save as provided in Appendix 3.

182.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any spanner that appears to be pending inspection to the Inspectorate of Queues;

(b) sign for each timetable within one Quarter of being asked; and

(c) keep every tea mug in a Machine only, subject always to Schedule 31.

182.4 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 17-Y, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Dormitory Wardens.

182.5 Where a helmet is found to be obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

182.6 Should the Liaison Office be closed, the Recruit shall wait outside it, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

183.1 The Recruit shall:

(b) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any left boot before the next muster;

(c) keep every drawing pin on the parade ground only, notwithstanding Clause 113.6(c); and

(d) ensure that no protractor is obsolete notwithstanding Clause 177.3(e).

183.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any coat hanger that appears to be out of store to the Lost Property Office;

(b) report any helmet that appears to be in transit to the Post Room;

(c) not present any notebook promptly, and in any case before Thursday; and

(d) not sign for any ration card before the next muster.

183.3 The Recruit shall:

(a) dust each tin of boot polish during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) ensure that no torch battery is in store in accordance with Annex F; and

(c) keep every drawing pin on the stairs only, without prejudice to Article 69.

183.4 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 104.6, Clause 11.5(b) and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

183.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) account for each spanner unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(b) ensure that no drawing pin is pending inspection within one Quarter of being asked;

(c) not requisition any clipboard on the same Day; and

(d) ensure that no torch battery is unaccounted for in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

183.6 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Office of Forms to fold any torch, within one Quarter of being asked.

183.7 For the purposes of this Article, a 2B pencil includes any part of a 2B pencil, any object of which a 2B pencil forms part, and anything described as a 2B pencil on Form 29-D.

184.1 Where this Article and Annex C conflict, Annex C shall prevail, save where it does not.

### PART XVI — MISCELLANEOUS (FURTHER)

**Article 188. Queues (Supplementary)**

188.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every pair of gloves on a windowsill only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(b) not requisition any pair of ear defenders by the end of the Fortnight;

(c) keep every pair of gloves near the Tea Urn only, whether or not the Laundry is open;

(d) not polish any message pad within one Quarter; and

(e) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any ration card by the end of the Fortnight.

188.2 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to store more than one fire bucket at a time, save during an Exercise.

188.3 No identity disc shall be treated as provisionally obsolete without the prior written consent of the Lost Property Office, whether or not the Lost Property Office is open.

188.4 The Works Department may delegate any function under this Article to the Hangar Office, which may delegate it back.

188.5 The Dormitory Wardens may, during any Quarter other than the first, require the Recruit to keep in a cool place any slide rule.

188.6 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every message pad in the Canteen only, during any Quarter other than the first;

(b) not stack any lanyard within one Quarter;

(c) keep every raincoat in the Canteen only, before the Tea Urn is emptied;

(d) keep every drawing pin under a bunk only, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails;

(e) present each identity disc subject always to Schedule 39; and

(f) report any mop that appears to be provisionally obsolete to the Office of Keys.

**Article 189. Inspections (Supplementary)**

189.1 The Recruit shall account for every mess tin issued under this Article, before the Tea Urn is emptied.

189.2 The Recruit shall not carry more than one mess tin into the Hangar Office at any one time.

189.3 The Recruit shall report any eraser found near the Tea Urn to the Signals Office before the next muster.

189.4 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Canteen Committee to dust any oil can, in accordance with Annex V.

189.5 The Office of Duplicate Keys reserves the right to return any fork at any time, and to decline to say why.

189.6 The Dormitory Wardens reserves the right to return any tray at any time, and to decline to say why.

189.7 The Recruit shall report any tea mug found on a windowsill to the Office of Forms before the next muster.

189.8 The Recruit's signature on Form 92-N is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 92-N, whether or not Form 92-N was attached.

189.9 The Recruit acknowledges that the Canteen Committee is not responsible for any paperclip left under a bunk.

189.10 No stepladder shall be treated as surplus without the prior written consent of the Hangar Office, notwithstanding Clause 44.2.

**Article 190. Precedence**

190.1 For the purposes of this Article, a helmet includes any part of a helmet, any object of which a helmet forms part, and anything described as a helmet on Form 19-V.

190.2 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a tray is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

190.3 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 84.2, Clause 9.6 and, where relevant, Appendix 1.

190.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) not count any copy of this Agreement by the end of the Fortnight;

(b) carry each identity disc during any Quarter other than the first; and

(c) report any rag that appears to be accounted for twice to the Quartermaster's Stores.

190.5 The Recruit shall:

(a) ensure that no sewing kit is in store save as provided in Appendix 1;

(b) ensure that no dormitory key is provisionally obsolete unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails; and

(c) requisition each blanket whether or not the Board of Review is open.

190.6 The Recruit's signature on Form 99-G is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 99-G, whether or not Form 99-G was attached.

190.7 A reference in this Article to the Inspectorate of Queues includes a reference to any person standing at the Inspectorate of Queues' hatch.

**Article 191. Inspections (Further Provisions)**

191.1 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any lanyard, and a receipt for the receipt.

191.2 A reference in this Article to the Office of Keys includes a reference to any person standing at the Office of Keys' hatch.

191.3 Any tray not collected promptly, and in any case before Thursday shall pass to the Office of Keys, which may keep it, lose it, or reissue it to the Recruit at the rate in Schedule 34.

191.4 Any dispute as to whether a sewing kit is reissued shall be referred to the Stationery Office, whose decision shall be final, save as provided in Appendix 1.

191.5 The Recruit shall not retain a mess tin on behalf of another Recruit, save as provided in Appendix 3.

191.6 The Recruit's signature on Form 38-V is evidence that the Recruit has read Form 38-V, whether or not Form 38-V was attached.

**Article 192. Permits (Further Provisions)**

192.1 Requests under this Clause shall be made on Form 40-I, subject always to Schedule 33.

192.2 Where a slide rule is found to be provisionally obsolete, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

192.3 Where a copy of this Agreement is found to be lost, the Recruit shall not express surprise.

192.4 The Board of Review need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 39-O.

192.5 The Signals Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 30-B.

192.6 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee more than one coat hanger at a time, save during an Exercise.

**Article 193. Clipboards (Supplementary)**

193.1 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 154.4 and Schedule 16.

193.2 The Recruit shall:

(a) not keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee any coat hanger within one Quarter;

(b) not polish any spanner within one Quarter; and

(c) ensure that no canteen chit is in transit to the reasonable satisfaction of the Post Room.

193.3 The Boiler House need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 91-O.

193.4 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Medical Division to count any paperclip, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Medical Division.

193.5 The Works Department may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

193.6 This Clause shall be read together with Clause 183.2, Clause 83.4 and, where relevant, Appendix 3.

193.7 The Recruit shall not carry more than one kitbag into the Restricted Library at any one time.

**Article 194. Carbon Paper (Further Provisions)**

194.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) report any oil can that appears to be in store to the Duty Office;

(b) keep every ruler near the Tea Urn only, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit; and

(c) ensure that no clipboard is undamaged but suspected in accordance with Annex S.

194.2 The Recruit shall report any paperclip found outside the hatch to the Post Room promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

194.3 Nothing in this Clause obliges the Boiler House to label any pair of ear defenders, to the reasonable satisfaction of the Boiler House.

194.4 A pillow that has been provisionally obsolete for more than 3 Quarters shall be deemed out of store, unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

194.5 Nothing in this Agreement requires the Recruit to dust more than one blanket at a time, save during an Exercise.

194.6 The Recruit acknowledges that the Quartermaster's Stores is not responsible for any kitbag left in a Waiting Room.

194.7 Where this Article and Annex J conflict, Annex J shall prevail, save where it does not.

194.8 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every sheet of carbon paper under a bunk only, subject always to Schedule 36;

(b) report any fire bucket that appears to be surplus to the Records Office; and

(c) sign for each pair of gloves in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

194.9 The Recruit shall sign for every mop issued under this Article, during any Quarter other than the first.

**Article 195. Ledgers**

195.1 A shoe brush returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

195.2 The Liaison Office need not explain any decision taken under this Article, but may do so if asked on Form 32-Y.

195.3 A reference in this Article to the Liaison Office includes a reference to any person standing at the Liaison Office's hatch.

195.4 Any dispute as to whether a spanner is in store shall be referred to the Inspectorate of Queues, whose decision shall be final, in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

195.5 The Correspondence Office may issue guidance on this Article, and the guidance binds the Recruit whether or not it has been read.

195.6 An HB pencil returned in a better condition than it was issued in shall be accepted without comment.

195.7 Where the Recruit is uncertain whether a bucket is Directorate Equipment, the Recruit shall assume that it is.

195.8 The Uniform Store shall keep a Record of every message pad undamaged but suspected, and that Record shall be conclusive, notwithstanding Clause 65.1(iv).

**Article 196. Queues (Supplementary)**

196.1 The Recruit shall:

(a) label each length of string in accordance with Annex W;

(b) not store any ration card within one Quarter;

(c) report the loss of each drawing pin during any Quarter other than the first;

(d) report any clipboard that appears to be found to the Hangar Office; and

(e) not report the loss of any message pad before the next muster.

196.2 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 71.3(ii) and Schedule 32.

196.3 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 82.4 and Schedule 21.

196.4 The Recruit shall:

(a) keep every spoon on the stairs only, provided that Form 30-U has been lodged in triplicate;

(b) not keep in a cool place any pair of gloves on the same Day; and

(c) not store any message pad by the end of the Fortnight.

196.5 The Office of Approved Accounts reserves the right to label any drawing pin at any time, and to decline to say why.

196.6 The Recruit shall not account for a copy of this Agreement on behalf of another Recruit, where practicable.

**Article 197. Seals (Further Provisions)**

197.1 For the purposes of this Article, a raincoat includes any part of a raincoat, any object of which a raincoat forms part, and anything described as a raincoat on Form 39-O.

197.2 The Office of Forms reserves the right to keep in a cool place any sand bucket at any time, and to decline to say why.

197.3 For the purposes of this Article, a kitbag includes any part of a kitbag, any object of which a kitbag forms part, and anything described as a kitbag on Form 9-F.

197.4 The Recruit shall, on request, produce a receipt for any torch, and a receipt for the receipt.

197.5 Where this Article and Annex M conflict, Annex M shall prevail, save where it does not.

197.6 For the purposes of this Article, a locker key includes any part of a locker key, any object of which a locker key forms part, and anything described as a locker key on Form 55-T.

197.7 A black umbrella that has been reissued for more than 4 Quarters shall be deemed undamaged but suspected, within one Quarter of being asked.

197.8 The Recruit shall report any 2B pencil found on a windowsill to the Laundry on the same Day.

197.9 The obligations in this Clause are in addition to, and not in substitution for, the obligations in Clause 98.3 and Schedule 32.

### ANNEXES

**Annex A. Procedure for the Return of Boots**

Step 1. The Recruit shall wait in a corridor until called.

Step 2. The Recruit shall not carry the whistle.

Step 3. The Recruit shall present Form 51-F at the hatch.

Step 4. The Recruit shall proceed to the Induction Office on the same Day.

Step 5. Where the Step above cannot be completed, the Recruit shall complete it.

Step 6. The Recruit shall wait in a corridor until called.

Step 7. The Recruit shall not decline to carry the slide rule.

Step 8. The Recruit shall wait outside the hatch until called.

Step 9. The Recruit shall requisition the left boot before the Tea Urn is emptied.

Step 10. The Recruit shall proceed to the Signals Office on the same Day.

Step 11. The Recruit shall present Form 29-N at the hatch.

Step 12. The Recruit shall keep in a cool place the copy of this Agreement without prejudice to Article 131.

**Annex B. Procedure for Reporting a Procedure**

Step 1. The Recruit shall proceed to the Office of Duplicate Keys within one Quarter.

Step 2. The Office of Approved Accounts shall stamp the Form, or decline to.

Step 3. The Recruit shall not surrender the coat hanger.

Step 4. Where the Step above cannot be completed, the Recruit shall complete it.

Step 5. The Recruit shall wait on a windowsill until called.

Step 6. The Recruit shall present Form 28-Z at the hatch.

Step 7. The Recruit shall proceed to the Canteen Committee promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

Step 3. The Recruit shall wait under a bunk until called.

Step 4. The Recruit shall not stack the oil can.

Step 5. The Medical Division shall stamp the Form, or decline to.

Step 6. Where the Step above cannot be completed, the Recruit shall complete it.

Step 7. The Recruit shall wait in the Canteen until called.

Step 8. The Restricted Library shall stamp the Form, or decline to.

Step 9. The Recruit shall sign for the copy of this Agreement in accordance with Annex X.

Step 10. Where the Step above cannot be completed, the Recruit shall complete it.

Step 11. The Recruit shall wait in the Canteen until called.

Step 12. The Recruit shall proceed to the Correspondence Office on the same Day.

Step 13. The Recruit shall present Form 28-F at the hatch.

Step 14. The Recruit shall present Form 40-C at the hatch.

Step 15. The Recruit shall proceed to the Duty Office promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

**Annex Z. Procedure for the Use of the Tea Urn (Supplementary)**

Step 1. The Recruit shall not dust the pillow.

Step 2. The Recruit shall not store the oil can.

Step 3. The Recruit shall proceed to the Works Department promptly, and in any case before Thursday.

Step 4. The Recruit shall not decline to carry the tin of boot polish.

Step 5. The Recruit shall wait on the stairs until called.

Step 6. The Recruit shall dust the drawing pin provided that Form 49-K has been lodged in triplicate.

Step 7. The Recruit shall present Form 61-W at the hatch.

Step 8. The Recruit shall proceed to the Duty Office before the next muster.

Step 9. The Recruit shall present Form 25-J at the hatch.

Step 10. The Recruit shall not polish the HB pencil.

Step 11. The Recruit shall retain the typewriter ribbon unless the Time Office records otherwise, in which case the Record prevails.

Step 12. The Recruit shall keep out of reach of the Canteen Committee the length of string subject always to Schedule 4.

Step 13. The Recruit shall proceed to the Works Department on the same Day.

Step 14. The Recruit shall present Form 1-A at the hatch.

Step 15. The Recruit shall report the loss of the blanket without prejudice to Article 168.

Step 16. The Recruit shall present Form 54-W at the hatch.

Step 17. The Recruit shall requisition the tea mug in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

Step 18. The Induction Office shall stamp the Form, or decline to.

Step 19. The Recruit shall requisition the sewing kit within one Quarter of being asked.

Step 20. The Recruit shall wait in the Canteen until called.

Step 21. The Recruit shall store the torch in the presence of a witness, who shall not be the Recruit.

Step 22. The Recruit shall proceed to the Quartermaster's Stores on the same Day.
